## [P01_identity_access] P01 — IDENTITY & ACCESS

**Вобастагӣ:** P00
**Мақсад:** User, бақайдгирӣ бо SMS OTP, login, JWT rotation, organizations-и базавӣ, membership, invitation, RBAC.

---

### 1. Доира

**Дохил:** users, OTP, tokens, `organizations` (ҷадвали умумӣ), memberships, invitations, permission registry, `/me`, org switcher.
**Берун:** профили Company/Store ва verification (P02).

---

### 2. Data Model

#### 2.1. `users`

| Field | Type | Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| id | UUID | PK |
| phone | VARCHAR(16) | NOT NULL, UNIQUE, CHECK формати E.164 (`^\+[1-9][0-9]{7,14}$`) |
| full_name | VARCHAR(150) | NOT NULL |
| password_hash | VARCHAR(255) | NOT NULL |
| language | VARCHAR(2) | CHECK IN (`tg`,`ru`,`en`), DEFAULT `tg` |
| status | VARCHAR(16) | CHECK IN (`ACTIVE`,`BLOCKED`) |
| is_superadmin | BOOLEAN | DEFAULT false |
| token_version | INTEGER | NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 |
| phone_verified_at | timestamptz | NOT NULL |
| last_login_at | timestamptz | NULL |
| created_at / updated_at | timestamptz | |

`is_superadmin` танҳо тавассути CLI (`python -m app.cli create-superadmin`) — API барои он нест.

#### 2.2. `otp_codes`

| Field | Type | Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| id | UUID | PK |
| phone | VARCHAR(16) | NOT NULL, INDEX |
| purpose | VARCHAR(16) | CHECK IN (`REGISTER`,`RESET_PASSWORD`) |
| code_hash | CHAR(64) | HMAC-SHA256(secret, phone+purpose+code) |
| attempts | SMALLINT | DEFAULT 0 |
| expires_at | timestamptz | NOT NULL |
| consumed_at | timestamptz | NULL |
| created_at | timestamptz | |

Қоидаҳо: код 6 рақам; TTL 5 дақиқа; 5 кӯшиш; resend баъд аз 60 сония; OTP-и нав OTP-ҳои кӯҳнаи ҳамон phone+purpose-ро беэътибор мекунад.

#### 2.3. `refresh_tokens`

| Field | Type | Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| id | UUID | PK (`jti`) |
| user_id | UUID | FK users |
| family_id | UUID | NOT NULL, INDEX |
| token_hash | CHAR(64) | UNIQUE (SHA-256) |
| expires_at | timestamptz | NOT NULL |
| revoked_at | timestamptz | NULL |
| replaced_by_id | UUID | NULL |
| ip | INET | NULL |
| user_agent | VARCHAR(512) | NULL |
| created_at | timestamptz | |

#### 2.4. `organizations`

| Field | Type | Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| id | UUID | PK |
| type | VARCHAR(8) | CHECK IN (`COMPANY`,`STORE`) |
| name | VARCHAR(200) | NOT NULL |
| status | VARCHAR(16) | CHECK IN (`ACTIVE`,`SUSPENDED`,`BLOCKED`), DEFAULT `ACTIVE` |
| created_by | UUID | FK users |
| created_at / updated_at | timestamptz | |
| version | INTEGER | |

Профилҳои муфассал (`companies`, `stores`) дар P02 бо `id = organizations.id` (1:1).

#### 2.5. `memberships`

| Field | Type | Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| id | UUID | PK |
| user_id | UUID | FK users |
| organization_id | UUID | FK organizations |
| role | VARCHAR(16) | CHECK IN (`OWNER`,`MANAGER`,`OPERATOR`,`WAREHOUSE`,`COURIER`,`SELLER`) |
| status | VARCHAR(16) | CHECK IN (`ACTIVE`,`SUSPENDED`,`REVOKED`) |
| joined_at | timestamptz | NOT NULL |
| suspended_at / revoked_at | timestamptz | NULL |
| invited_by | UUID | NULL FK users |
| created_at / updated_at / version | | |

Constraints:
- Partial UNIQUE (`user_id`, `organization_id`) WHERE `status <> 'REVOKED'`.
- Partial UNIQUE (`organization_id`) WHERE `role = 'OWNER' AND status = 'ACTIVE'` — як OWNER.
- Role мувофиқи навъи org: COMPANY → `OWNER, MANAGER, OPERATOR, WAREHOUSE, COURIER`; STORE → `OWNER, SELLER` (санҷиш дар service ва trigger).

#### 2.6. `membership_invitations`

| Field | Type | Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| id | UUID | PK |
| organization_id | UUID | FK |
| phone | VARCHAR(16) | NOT NULL |
| role | VARCHAR(16) | (OWNER манъ) |
| status | VARCHAR(16) | CHECK IN (`PENDING`,`ACCEPTED`,`DECLINED`,`REVOKED`,`EXPIRED`) |
| invited_by | UUID | FK users |
| expires_at | timestamptz | created_at + 7 рӯз |
| responded_at | timestamptz | NULL |
| created_at | timestamptz | |

Partial UNIQUE (`organization_id`, `phone`) WHERE `status = 'PENDING'`.

---

### 3. State machines

#### 3.1. Membership

| From | To | Actor | Шартҳо | Audit |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| — | ACTIVE | System | Invitation accept ё сохтани org (OWNER) | `membership.created` |
| ACTIVE | SUSPENDED | OWNER | target ≠ OWNER, target ≠ self | `membership.suspended` |
| SUSPENDED | ACTIVE | OWNER | P03: лимити `users` | `membership.reactivated` |
| ACTIVE, SUSPENDED | REVOKED | OWNER | target ≠ OWNER | `membership.revoked` |
| ACTIVE | REVOKED | Self (leave) | role ≠ OWNER | `membership.left` |

REVOKED — ниҳоӣ. Амалҳои таърихии user боқӣ мемонанд.

#### 3.2. Invitation

| From | To | Actor |
|---|---|---|
| PENDING | ACCEPTED | User бо ҳамон phone |
| PENDING | DECLINED | User бо ҳамон phone |
| PENDING | REVOKED | OWNER |
| PENDING | EXPIRED | System (Celery ҳар соат) |

---

### 4. Business rules

| ID | Қоида |
|---|---|
| IAM-001 | Бақайдгирӣ: `register/start` ҳамеша `202` бармегардонад (enumeration манъ). Агар phone аллакай бақайд бошад, SMS-и «шумо аллакай ҳисоб доред» фиристода мешавад, OTP не. |
| IAM-002 | `register/verify` бо OTP-и дуруст `registration_token` медиҳад (JWT, `typ=registration`, 15 дақиқа, як истифода — `jti` дар Redis). |
| IAM-003 | `register/complete`: password ≥ 8 аломат, ақаллан як ҳарф ва як рақам, на аз рӯйхати 10k password-ҳои маъмул → вагарна `weak_password`. |
| IAM-004 | Login: хатои phone ё password — ҳамон `invalid_credentials` ва тақрибан ҳамон вақт (dummy hash барои phone-и номаълум). |
| IAM-005 | Access JWT: `sub`, `sid` (family_id), `tv` (token_version), `typ=access`, `exp` 15 дақ. Ҳар request `user.status = ACTIVE` ва `tv = users.token_version`-ро месанҷад. |
| IAM-006 | Refresh rotation: ҳар `/auth/refresh` token-и кӯҳнаро `revoked_at` + `replaced_by_id` мекунад ва нав медиҳад (ҳамон family). |
| IAM-007 | Reuse detection: refresh-и аллакай revoked пешниҳод шавад → тамоми family revoke, `401 refresh_token_reused`, audit `auth.refresh_reuse`. |
| IAM-008 | Logout: family-и ҷорӣ revoke. Logout-all ва password change: `token_version++` ва ҳамаи refresh-ҳо revoke. |
| IAM-009 | Block-и user (SUPERADMIN, P12): `status=BLOCKED`, `token_version++`. |
| IAM-010 | `X-Org-Id` барои endpoint-ҳои org-scoped ҳатмӣ; membership бояд `ACTIVE` ва `organizations.status ≠ BLOCKED` бошад. |
| IAM-011 | Танҳо OWNER role таъин/иваз мекунад (DEC-18). Role-и худро иваз кардан манъ. Role-и OWNER-ро тавассути API додан манъ. |
| IAM-012 | Invitation танҳо ба phone; accept танҳо аз ҷониби user бо ҳамон `users.phone` → вагарна `invitation_phone_mismatch`. |
| IAM-013 | Accept: агар membership-и ғайри-REVOKED бо org аллакай бошад → `membership_already_exists`. |
| IAM-014 | Revoked/suspended membership дар request-и навбатӣ дарҳол `403 membership_inactive` мегирад (кеш нест). |
| IAM-015 | Password reset: OTP → `reset_token` (15 дақ, як истифода) → password-и нав → `token_version++`. |
| IAM-016 | OTP SMS тавассути `SmsPort`: `ConsoleSmsProvider` дар dev/test, provider-и воқеӣ дар P11. OTP мустақиман фиристода мешавад (на тавассути outbox), вале бо timeout 5 сония; ноком → `503 service_unavailable`. |

---

### 5. Permission registry

`app/core/permissions.py`:

```python
PERMISSIONS: dict[Role, frozenset[str]]   # ҳар қисм кодҳои худро илова мекунад
def require(permission: str) -> Depends   # 403 permission_denied
```

Permission code формат: `<module>.<action>` (масалан `members.invite`). `/me` рӯйхати permission-ҳоро барои ҳар membership бармегардонад.

#### 5.1. Матритса (P01)

| Permission | C.OWNER | C.MANAGER | C.OPERATOR | C.WAREHOUSE | C.COURIER | S.OWNER | S.SELLER |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `members.view` | ✓ | ✓ | — | — | — | ✓ | — |
| `members.invite` | ✓ | — | — | — | — | ✓ | — |
| `members.change_role` | ✓ | — | — | — | — | ✓ | — |
| `members.suspend` | ✓ | — | — | — | — | ✓ | — |
| `members.revoke` | ✓ | — | — | — | — | ✓ | — |

---

### 6. API

| Method | Path | Auth | Idempotent | Тавсиф |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| POST | `/api/v1/auth/register/start` | — | — | `{phone}` → 202 |
| POST | `/api/v1/auth/register/verify` | — | — | `{phone, code}` → `{registration_token}` |
| POST | `/api/v1/auth/register/complete` | reg token | — | `{registration_token, full_name, password, language}` → 201 + tokens |
| POST | `/api/v1/auth/login` | — | — | `{phone, password}` → `{access_token, expires_in, user}` + cookie refresh |
| POST | `/api/v1/auth/refresh` | cookie + CSRF | — | → access нав + cookie нав |
| POST | `/api/v1/auth/logout` | cookie + CSRF | — | 204 |
| POST | `/api/v1/auth/logout-all` | access | — | 204 |
| POST | `/api/v1/auth/password/reset/start` | — | — | 202 |
| POST | `/api/v1/auth/password/reset/verify` | — | — | → `{reset_token}` |
| POST | `/api/v1/auth/password/reset/complete` | — | — | 204 |
| POST | `/api/v1/auth/password/change` | access | — | `{current_password, new_password}` → 204 |
| GET | `/api/v1/me` | access | — | user + memberships (+ `permissions`, `org_type`, `org_name`, `org_status`) |
| PATCH | `/api/v1/me` | access | — | `{full_name?, language?}` |
| GET | `/api/v1/me/invitations` | access | — | Invitation-ҳои PENDING барои phone-и ман |
| POST | `/api/v1/me/invitations/{id}/accept` | access | ✓ | → membership |
| POST | `/api/v1/me/invitations/{id}/decline` | access | — | |
| GET | `/api/v1/members` | org | — | `members.view`; filter: `role`, `status`; search: `full_name`, `phone` |
| POST | `/api/v1/members/invitations` | org | ✓ | `members.invite`; `{phone, role}` |
| GET | `/api/v1/members/invitations` | org | — | `members.view` |
| POST | `/api/v1/members/invitations/{id}/revoke` | org | — | `members.invite` |
| PATCH | `/api/v1/members/{id}` | org | — | `members.change_role`; `{role, version}` |
| POST | `/api/v1/members/{id}/suspend` | org | — | `{reason}` |
| POST | `/api/v1/members/{id}/reactivate` | org | — | |
| POST | `/api/v1/members/{id}/revoke` | org | — | `{reason}` |
| POST | `/api/v1/members/leave` | org | — | self |

Rate limits: GLOBAL/P00 §4.1.

---

### 7. Audit (ҳатмӣ)

`auth.login`, `auth.login_failed` (бе password), `auth.logout`, `auth.logout_all`, `auth.refresh_reuse`, `auth.password_changed`, `auth.password_reset`, `membership.created`, `membership.role_changed`, `membership.suspended`, `membership.reactivated`, `membership.revoked`, `membership.left`, `invitation.created`, `invitation.revoked`, `invitation.accepted`.

### 8. Domain events

`USER_REGISTERED`, `MEMBERSHIP_CREATED`, `MEMBERSHIP_ROLE_CHANGED`, `MEMBERSHIP_REVOKED`, `INVITATION_CREATED` (→ P11: SMS ба phone).

### 9. Ports (барои қисмҳои оянда)

| Port | Default дар P01 | Implementation |
|---|---|---|
| `SubscriptionGuardPort.check_limit(org_id, "users")` | ҳамеша OK | P03 |
| `SmsPort.send(phone, text)` | `ConsoleSmsProvider` | P11 |

---

### 10. Frontend

| Экран | Route | Тавсиф |
|---|---|---|
| Login | `/login` | phone (маска `+992`), password; линк ба register ва reset |
| Register | `/register` | 3 қадам: phone → OTP (таймери resend 60 с) → ном + password + забон |
| Password reset | `/reset` | 3 қадам |
| No organization | `/welcome` | Агар membership нест: «Ширкат созед» / «Мағоза созед» (P02) ва рӯйхати invitation-ҳо |
| Org switcher | header | Рӯйхати membership-ҳо; интихоб → `X-Org-Id` + гузариш ба area-и мувофиқ |
| Profile | `/profile` | ном, забон, иваз кардани password, logout-all |
| Team | `/company/team`, `/store/team` | Ҷадвал, invite dialog (phone + role), иваз кардани role, suspend/revoke бо тасдиқ |
| Invitations inbox | `/welcome`, header badge | accept/decline |

Auth store (Zustand): `accessToken` (memory), `user`, `activeOrgId` (`localStorage`). Refresh: FE-008. Guard-ҳо: `RequireAuth`, `RequireOrgType('COMPANY'|'STORE')`, `RequirePermission(code)`.

---

### 11. Tests (ҳадди ақал)

```text
test_iam_001_register_start_always_202
test_iam_002_register_verify_wrong_code_attempts_decrement / _expired / _locked_after_5
test_iam_003_weak_password_rejected
test_iam_004_login_invalid_credentials_same_error
test_iam_005_access_token_rejected_after_token_version_bump
test_iam_006_refresh_rotation
test_iam_007_refresh_reuse_revokes_family
test_iam_008_logout_all_revokes_everything
test_iam_010_org_header_required / _foreign_org_404 / _blocked_org_403
test_iam_011_only_owner_changes_role (parametrized ҳамаи role-ҳо)
test_iam_011_cannot_change_own_role / cannot_grant_owner
test_iam_012_invitation_phone_mismatch
test_iam_013_duplicate_membership
test_iam_014_revoked_membership_immediate_403
test_iam_015_password_reset_flow
test_membership_single_owner_constraint
test_membership_role_matches_org_type
test_permission_matrix_p01 (ҳар катак)
test_rate_limit_login_5_per_15min
```

---

### 12. Part Acceptance

```text
[ ] Register → login → refresh → logout кор мекунанд (API + UI)
[ ] Reuse-и refresh family-ро revoke мекунад
[ ] OWNER invite мекунад, user accept мекунад, role иваз мешавад, revoke → 403 дарҳол
[ ] Матритсаи permission пурра тест шудааст
```

## [P02_organizations] P02 — ORGANIZATIONS & VERIFICATION

**Вобастагӣ:** P00, P01
**Мақсад:** сохтани Company ва Store, профилҳо, файлҳо, lifecycle-и verification ва навбати verification барои SUPERADMIN.

---

### 1. Data Model

#### 1.1. `companies` (1:1 бо `organizations`)

| Field | Type | Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| id | UUID | PK, FK `organizations.id` |
| legal_name | VARCHAR(255) | NOT NULL |
| tax_identifier | VARCHAR(32) | NOT NULL, UNIQUE (ИНН) |
| public_code | CHAR(8) | NOT NULL, UNIQUE — A–Z и 2–9 (бе `0,O,1,I`); барои дархости partnership аз ҷониби store |
| phone | VARCHAR(16) | NOT NULL, E.164 |
| email | VARCHAR(254) | NULL |
| city | VARCHAR(100) | NOT NULL |
| address | VARCHAR(500) | NOT NULL |
| verification_status | VARCHAR(16) | CHECK IN (`NOT_SUBMITTED`,`PENDING`,`APPROVED`,`REJECTED`), DEFAULT `NOT_SUBMITTED` |
| verified_at | timestamptz | NULL |
| updated_at / version | | |

#### 1.2. `stores` (1:1 бо `organizations`)

| Field | Type | Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| id | UUID | PK, FK `organizations.id` |
| legal_name | VARCHAR(255) | NOT NULL |
| tax_identifier | VARCHAR(32) | NULL, UNIQUE (WHERE NOT NULL) |
| phone | VARCHAR(16) | NOT NULL |
| email | VARCHAR(254) | NULL |
| city | VARCHAR(100) | NOT NULL |
| address | VARCHAR(500) | NOT NULL |
| latitude | NUMERIC(9,6) | NULL, CHECK BETWEEN -90 AND 90 |
| longitude | NUMERIC(9,6) | NULL, CHECK BETWEEN -180 AND 180 |
| verification_status | VARCHAR(16) | ҳамон қиматҳо |
| verified_at | timestamptz | NULL |
| updated_at / version | | |

CHECK: `(latitude IS NULL) = (longitude IS NULL)` — ё ҳарду, ё ҳеҷ.

#### 1.3. `stored_files`

| Field | Type | Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| id | UUID | PK |
| organization_id | UUID | FK |
| category | VARCHAR(32) | CHECK IN (`VERIFICATION`,`IMPORT`,`EXPORT`,`PRODUCT_IMAGE`) |
| storage_key | VARCHAR(512) | UNIQUE |
| content_type | VARCHAR(100) | NOT NULL |
| size_bytes | BIGINT | CHECK > 0 |
| sha256 | CHAR(64) | NOT NULL |
| display_name | VARCHAR(255) | санитизатсияшуда; танҳо барои намоиш |
| uploaded_by | UUID | FK users |
| created_at | timestamptz | |

#### 1.4. `verification_requests`

| Field | Type | Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| id | UUID | PK |
| organization_id | UUID | FK |
| status | VARCHAR(16) | CHECK IN (`SUBMITTED`,`UNDER_REVIEW`,`APPROVED`,`REJECTED`) |
| submitted_by | UUID | FK users |
| submitted_at | timestamptz | NOT NULL |
| reviewer_id | UUID | NULL FK users |
| review_started_at | timestamptz | NULL |
| reviewed_at | timestamptz | NULL |
| rejection_reason | TEXT | NULL; CHECK: ҳатмӣ агар `REJECTED` |
| legal_snapshot | JSONB | NOT NULL — legal_name, tax_identifier, address дар вақти submit |
| created_at / updated_at / version | | |

Partial UNIQUE (`organization_id`) WHERE `status IN ('SUBMITTED','UNDER_REVIEW')`.

#### 1.5. `verification_documents`

| Field | Type | Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| id | UUID | PK |
| request_id | UUID | FK verification_requests |
| doc_type | VARCHAR(32) | CHECK IN (`REGISTRATION_CERTIFICATE`,`TAX_CERTIFICATE`,`OTHER`) |
| file_id | UUID | FK stored_files |
| created_at | timestamptz | |

---

### 2. State machines

#### 2.1. Verification request

| From | To | Actor | Шартҳо | Side effects |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| — | SUBMITTED | Org OWNER | Ҳуҷҷатҳои ҳатмӣ (VER-003); ягон request-и кушода нест | org.verification_status = `PENDING`; event `VERIFICATION_SUBMITTED` |
| SUBMITTED | UNDER_REVIEW | SUPERADMIN | — | `reviewer_id`, `review_started_at` |
| UNDER_REVIEW | APPROVED | SUPERADMIN (ҳамон reviewer) | — | org.verification_status = `APPROVED`, `verified_at`; event `VERIFICATION_APPROVED` |
| UNDER_REVIEW | REJECTED | SUPERADMIN | `rejection_reason` ≥ 10 аломат | org.verification_status = `REJECTED`; event `VERIFICATION_REJECTED` |

Пас аз REJECTED OWNER request-и **нав** месозад. Ҳама transition-ҳо audit: `verification.<to_lower>`.

#### 2.2. Organization status

`ACTIVE ↔ SUSPENDED`, `ACTIVE|SUSPENDED → BLOCKED`, `BLOCKED → ACTIVE` — танҳо SUPERADMIN (P12), бо reason. SUSPENDED: хондан иҷозат, тағйир манъ (`organization_blocked`). BLOCKED: ҳама чиз манъ ба ҷуз `/me`.

---

### 3. Business rules

| ID | Қоида |
|---|---|
| ORG-001 | `POST /organizations/companies` дар як transaction: `organizations` + `companies` + `memberships(OWNER, ACTIVE)` + `public_code` (генератсия бо retry ҳангоми collision) + event `COMPANY_CREATED`. |
| ORG-002 | `POST /organizations/stores` ҳамин тавр бо `stores` + event `STORE_CREATED`. |
| ORG-003 | Як user метавонад чанд organization созад. Ҳадди аксар 5 organization, ки user OWNER-и онҳост (зидди spam). |
| ORG-004 | `tax_identifier`: 9–12 рақам (формати ИНН-и Тоҷикистон); unique. |
| ORG-005 | Пас аз `APPROVED` field-ҳои `legal_name`, `tax_identifier` таҳрир намешаванд (`verification_not_editable`); танҳо SUPERADMIN бо reason (audit). Field-ҳои дигар (phone, address, coordinates) таҳрир мешаванд — audit `organization.updated`. |
| ORG-006 | Профилро OWNER таҳрир мекунад; MANAGER танҳо `phone, email, address, city, coordinates`. |
| VER-001 | Verification танҳо аз ҷониби OWNER submit мешавад. |
| VER-002 | Файл: `application/pdf`, `image/jpeg`, `image/png`; ≤ 10 MB; magic bytes мувофиқи content type; SHA-256 ҳисоб мешавад. |
| VER-003 | Ҳуҷҷатҳои ҳатмӣ: Company — `REGISTRATION_CERTIFICATE` + `TAX_CERTIFICATE`; Store — `REGISTRATION_CERTIFICATE`. Вагарна `verification_documents_missing`. |
| VER-004 | Ҳуҷҷатҳо private; дастрасӣ танҳо ба OWNER-и org ва SUPERADMIN тавассути signed URL (5 дақиқа). Ҳар дастрасии SUPERADMIN audit: `verification.document_viewed`. |
| VER-005 | Ҳуҷҷатҳо ва request-ҳо delete намешаванд (retention: P13). |
| VER-006 | Шарти DEC-19: partnership-и `ACTIVE` танҳо агар ҳарду `APPROVED` (санҷиш дар P06). Catalog, price, stock бе verification сохта мешаванд. |

---

### 4. Permission matrix (P02)

| Permission | C.OWNER | C.MANAGER | C.OPERATOR | C.WAREHOUSE | C.COURIER | S.OWNER | S.SELLER | SUPERADMIN |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `org.view` | ✓ | ✓ | ✓ | ✓ | ✓ | ✓ | ✓ | ✓ |
| `org.edit_contacts` | ✓ | ✓ | — | — | — | ✓ | — | ✓ |
| `org.edit_legal` | ✓ (то APPROVED) | — | — | — | — | ✓ (то APPROVED) | — | ✓ |
| `verification.submit` | ✓ | — | — | — | — | ✓ | — | — |
| `verification.view` | ✓ | ✓ | — | — | — | ✓ | — | ✓ |
| `verification.review` | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | ✓ |

---

### 5. API

| Method | Path | Permission | Idempotent | Тавсиф |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| POST | `/api/v1/organizations/companies` | authenticated | ✓ | Сохтани company |
| POST | `/api/v1/organizations/stores` | authenticated | ✓ | Сохтани store |
| GET | `/api/v1/organization` | `org.view` | — | Профили org-и ҷорӣ (`X-Org-Id`) |
| PATCH | `/api/v1/organization` | `org.edit_*` | — | `version` ҳатмӣ |
| POST | `/api/v1/files` | org member | — | multipart `file`, `category` → `{id, display_name, size_bytes, content_type}` |
| GET | `/api/v1/files/{id}/url` | мувофиқи category | — | `{url, expires_at}` |
| GET | `/api/v1/verification` | `verification.view` | — | Ҳолати ҷорӣ + request-и охирин + ҳуҷҷатҳо |
| POST | `/api/v1/verification` | `verification.submit` | ✓ | `{documents: [{doc_type, file_id}]}` |
| GET | `/api/v1/admin/verifications` | SUPERADMIN | — | filter: `status`, `org_type`; ordering: `submitted_at` |
| GET | `/api/v1/admin/verifications/{id}` | SUPERADMIN | — | бо legal_snapshot ва ҳуҷҷатҳо |
| POST | `/api/v1/admin/verifications/{id}/start-review` | SUPERADMIN | — | |
| POST | `/api/v1/admin/verifications/{id}/approve` | SUPERADMIN | — | |
| POST | `/api/v1/admin/verifications/{id}/reject` | SUPERADMIN | — | `{reason}` |

Admin endpoint-ҳо `X-Org-Id` талаб намекунанд; dependency `require_superadmin`.

---

### 6. Domain events

`COMPANY_CREATED` (→ P03: trial), `STORE_CREATED`, `VERIFICATION_SUBMITTED` (→ P11: SUPERADMIN), `VERIFICATION_APPROVED`, `VERIFICATION_REJECTED` (→ P11: OWNER).

### 7. Ports

| Port | Default | Implementation |
|---|---|---|
| `SubscriptionStarterPort.start_trial(company_id)` — handler-и `COMPANY_CREATED` | no-op | P03 |

---

### 8. Frontend

| Экран | Route | Тавсиф |
|---|---|---|
| Сохтани company | `/welcome/company` | Form: name, legal_name, tax_identifier, phone, city, address → area-и company |
| Сохтани store | `/welcome/store` | Form + интихоби координата: тугмаи «ҷойи ман» (Geolocation API) ё ворид кардани дастӣ; харита нест (DEC: map provider нест) |
| Профил | `/company/settings/profile`, `/store/settings/profile` | Таҳрир мувофиқи permission; field-ҳои legal пас аз APPROVED — readonly бо тавзеҳ |
| Verification | `/company/settings/verification`, `/store/settings/verification` | Ҳолат (badge), рӯйхати ҳуҷҷатҳои ҳатмӣ бо upload (progress, validation-и client: андоза ва навъ), submit бо тасдиқ; агар REJECTED — сабаб ва тугмаи «аз нав фиристодан» |
| Баннери verification | ҳамаи саҳифаҳои company/store | Агар ≠ APPROVED: «Барои фаъол кардани партнёршип verification лозим аст» |
| Admin: навбат | `/admin/verifications` | Ҷадвал бо filter; кушодани request: legal_snapshot, preview-и ҳуҷҷатҳо (PDF/image дар modal), тугмаҳои start-review / approve / reject (reject — reason ҳатмӣ) |

---

```text
test_org_002_create_store
```

## [P03_subscriptions] P03 — SUBSCRIPTIONS & LIMITS

**Вобастагӣ:** P00–P02
**Мақсад:** планҳо, lifecycle-и subscription-и Company, пардохти дастии subscription (SUPERADMIN), `SubscriptionGuard` ва лимитҳо, ки ҳамаи қисмҳои оянда истифода мебаранд.

Store subscription надорад (DEC: Store ройгон).

---

### 1. Data Model

#### 1.1. `subscription_plans`

| Field | Type | Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| id | UUID | PK |
| code | VARCHAR(16) | UNIQUE (`START`,`STANDARD`,`LARGE`, …) |
| name | JSONB | `{"tg": "...", "ru": "...", "en": "..."}` |
| price_monthly | NUMERIC(12,2) | CHECK ≥ 0 |
| currency | CHAR(3) | `TJS` |
| max_active_stores | INTEGER | NULL = бемаҳдуд; CHECK > 0 |
| max_users | INTEGER | NULL = бемаҳдуд |
| max_products | INTEGER | NULL = бемаҳдуд |
| is_public | BOOLEAN | DEFAULT true |
| sort_order | SMALLINT | |
| created_at / updated_at / version | | |

Seed (қиматҳо **намунавӣ** — SUPERADMIN дар P12 иваз мекунад):

| code | price_monthly | stores | users | products |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| START | 300.00 | 30 | 5 | 500 |
| STANDARD | 700.00 | 150 | 15 | 3000 |
| LARGE | 1500.00 | NULL | NULL | NULL |

Тағйири нарх ба давраи аллакай пардохтшуда таъсир намерасонад.

#### 1.2. `subscriptions`

| Field | Type | Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| id | UUID | PK |
| company_id | UUID | FK companies, UNIQUE |
| plan_id | UUID | FK subscription_plans |
| status | VARCHAR(16) | CHECK IN (`TRIAL`,`ACTIVE`,`GRACE`,`SOFT_BLOCK`,`FULL_BLOCK`,`CANCELLED`) |
| trial_ends_at | timestamptz | NULL |
| current_period_start | timestamptz | NULL |
| current_period_end | timestamptz | NULL |
| grace_ends_at | timestamptz | NULL |
| soft_block_ends_at | timestamptz | NULL |
| cancel_at_period_end | BOOLEAN | DEFAULT false |
| status_changed_at | timestamptz | NOT NULL |
| created_at / updated_at / version | | |

Index: (`status`, `trial_ends_at`), (`status`, `current_period_end`), (`status`, `grace_ends_at`), (`status`, `soft_block_ends_at`).

#### 1.3. `subscription_payments` (append-only)

| Field | Type | Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| id | UUID | PK |
| subscription_id | UUID | FK |
| plan_id | UUID | FK — план дар вақти пардохт |
| months | SMALLINT | CHECK BETWEEN 1 AND 12 |
| amount | NUMERIC(12,2) | CHECK > 0 |
| currency | CHAR(3) | |
| method | VARCHAR(16) | CHECK IN (`CASH`,`BANK_TRANSFER`) |
| reference | VARCHAR(100) | NULL (рақами ҳуҷҷати бонкӣ) |
| period_start | timestamptz | NOT NULL |
| period_end | timestamptz | NOT NULL |
| recorded_by | UUID | FK users (SUPERADMIN) |
| created_at | timestamptz | |

#### 1.4. `subscription_status_history` (append-only)

`id, subscription_id, from_status, to_status, reason, actor_id NULL, created_at`.

#### 1.5. `subscription_reminders`

`id, subscription_id, kind, anchor_at, created_at` — UNIQUE (`subscription_id`, `kind`, `anchor_at`). Барои idempotency-и reminder-ҳо.

#### 1.6. `plan_change_requests`

`id, company_id, requested_plan_id, requested_by, status (PENDING|DONE|DISMISSED), created_at, handled_by, handled_at`.

---

### 2. State machine

| From | To | Trigger | Шарт |
|---|---|---|---|
| — | TRIAL | Event `COMPANY_CREATED` | plan = `STANDARD`, `trial_ends_at = now + 14 рӯз` |
| TRIAL | ACTIVE | Сабти пардохт (SUPERADMIN) | — |
| TRIAL | GRACE | Job | `now ≥ trial_ends_at`; `grace_ends_at = trial_ends_at + 7 рӯз` |
| ACTIVE | GRACE | Job | `now ≥ current_period_end` ва `cancel_at_period_end = false` |
| ACTIVE | CANCELLED | Job | `now ≥ current_period_end` ва `cancel_at_period_end = true` |
| GRACE | ACTIVE | Сабти пардохт | — |
| GRACE | SOFT_BLOCK | Job | `now ≥ grace_ends_at`; `soft_block_ends_at = grace_ends_at + 14 рӯз` |
| SOFT_BLOCK | ACTIVE | Сабти пардохт | — |
| SOFT_BLOCK | FULL_BLOCK | Job | `now ≥ soft_block_ends_at` |
| FULL_BLOCK | ACTIVE | Сабти пардохт | — |
| CANCELLED | ACTIVE | Сабти пардохт | `cancel_at_period_end = false` мешавад |

Ҳар transition: `subscription_status_history` + audit `subscription.status_changed` + event `SUBSCRIPTION_STATUS_CHANGED`.

#### 2.1. Ҳисоби давра ҳангоми пардохт (SUB-004)

```text
if status == ACTIVE and current_period_end > now:
    period_start = current_period_end
else:
    period_start = now
period_end = period_start + months (календарӣ; 31 янв + 1 моҳ = 28/29 фев)
```

---

### 3. Матритсаи маҳдудиятҳо (SUB-006)

Категорияҳои амал (`SubAction`):

| SubAction | TRIAL / ACTIVE / GRACE | SOFT_BLOCK | FULL_BLOCK / CANCELLED |
|---|---|---|---|
| `READ` | ✓ | ✓ | ✓ |
| `EXPORT` | ✓ | ✓ | ✓ |
| `PAYMENT` (сабт/тасдиқи пардохти store) | ✓ | ✓ | ✓ |
| `NEW_ORDER` (store ба ин company order месозад) | ✓ | ✗ | ✗ |
| `ORDER_FULFILLMENT` (confirm, assemble, dispatch) | ✓ | ✓ | ✗ |
| `ORDER_CLOSE` (cancel, deliver/fail аз IN_TRANSIT) | ✓ | ✓ | ✓ |
| `CATALOG_WRITE` (product, price, stock) | ✓ | ✓ | ✗ |
| `PARTNERSHIP_NEW` (invite, activate) | ✓ | ✗ | ✗ |
| `MEMBER_INVITE` | ✓ | ✗ | ✗ |
| `RETURNS_DISPUTES` | ✓ | ✓ | ✓ |

Маълумот ҳеҷ гоҳ барои ҷазо нест карда намешавад (SUB-007).

---

### 4. Business rules

| ID | Қоида |
|---|---|
| SUB-001 | Company ҳамеша маҳз як subscription дорад (UNIQUE `company_id`). |
| SUB-002 | Trial автоматӣ ҳангоми `COMPANY_CREATED` (handler-и синхронӣ). |
| SUB-003 | Пардохтро танҳо SUPERADMIN сабт мекунад (DEC-20); `amount` бояд `plan.price_monthly × months` бошад, ё SUPERADMIN бо `override_amount_reason` фарқиятро тасдиқ мекунад (audit). |
| SUB-004 | Ҳисоби давра — §2.1. Пардохт `Idempotency-Key` талаб мекунад. |
| SUB-005 | Job `subscription_tick` ҳар 15 дақиқа: ҳар subscription-и мувофиқ бо `SELECT … FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED`, transition тибқи §2. Такрори job натиҷаи дуюм намедиҳад. |
| SUB-006 | `SubscriptionGuard.require(company_id, SubAction)` → `403 subscription_blocked` бо `details: {status, company_id}`. Ҳамаи қисмҳои оянда онро дар service даъват мекунанд. |
| SUB-007 | Ҳеҷ status маълумотро нест намекунад. |
| SUB-008 | Лимитҳо: `SubscriptionGuard.check_limit(company_id, kind, adding=1)` → `403 subscription_limit_reached` бо `details {limit, current, max}`. Нуқтаҳои enforcement: `active_stores` — P06 activation; `users` — P01 invitation accept ва reactivate; `products` — P04 create/import/activate. |
| SUB-009 | Downgrade (SUPERADMIN): иҷозат ҳатто агар истифода > лимит; объектҳои мавҷуда боқӣ мемонанд, иловаи нав манъ то истифода < лимит. |
| SUB-010 | Reminders (event `SUBSCRIPTION_EXPIRING`): 7, 3, 1 рӯз пеш аз `trial_ends_at` / `current_period_end`; ва ҳангоми ворид шудан ба GRACE, SOFT_BLOCK, FULL_BLOCK. Idempotent тавассути `subscription_reminders`. |
| SUB-011 | OWNER метавонад `cancel_at_period_end` гузорад/бекор кунад (audit). |
| SUB-012 | OWNER метавонад дархости иваз кардани план фиристад → `plan_change_requests` + event барои SUPERADMIN. |

---

### 5. Permission matrix (P03)

| Permission | C.OWNER | C.MANAGER | Дигарон | SUPERADMIN |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `subscription.view` | ✓ | ✓ | — | ✓ |
| `subscription.manage` (cancel, plan request) | ✓ | — | — | ✓ |
| `subscription.record_payment` | — | — | — | ✓ |
| `plans.manage` | — | — | — | ✓ |

---

### 6. API

| Method | Path | Permission | Idempotent |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/v1/plans` | authenticated | — |
| GET | `/api/v1/subscription` | `subscription.view` — ҳолат, план, санаҳо, истифода/лимитҳо (`usage: {active_stores, users, products}`), `allowed_actions` | — |
| POST | `/api/v1/subscription/cancel-at-period-end` | `subscription.manage` `{value: bool}` | — |
| POST | `/api/v1/subscription/plan-requests` | `subscription.manage` `{plan_code}` | ✓ |
| GET | `/api/v1/subscription/payments` | `subscription.view` | — |
| GET | `/api/v1/admin/subscriptions` | SUPERADMIN; filter `status`, `plan`; search company name | — |
| GET | `/api/v1/admin/subscriptions/{id}` | SUPERADMIN (+ history, payments) | — |
| POST | `/api/v1/admin/subscriptions/{id}/payments` | SUPERADMIN `{months, amount, method, reference?, override_amount_reason?}` | ✓ |
| POST | `/api/v1/admin/subscriptions/{id}/change-plan` | SUPERADMIN `{plan_code, reason}` | — |
| POST | `/api/v1/admin/subscriptions/{id}/extend-trial` | SUPERADMIN `{days ≤ 30, reason}` (танҳо дар TRIAL) | — |
| GET/POST/PATCH | `/api/v1/admin/plans` | SUPERADMIN | — |
| GET | `/api/v1/admin/plan-requests` | SUPERADMIN | — |

---

### 7. Celery tasks

`subscription_tick` (ҳар 15 дақ), `subscription_reminders` (ҳар соат), `expire_invitations` (аз P01, ҳар соат — агар ҳанӯз насб нашуда бошад).

### 8. Domain events

`SUBSCRIPTION_STATUS_CHANGED`, `SUBSCRIPTION_EXPIRING`, `SUBSCRIPTION_PAYMENT_RECORDED`, `PLAN_CHANGE_REQUESTED`.

### 9. Ports implemented

`SubscriptionGuardPort` (P01), `SubscriptionStarterPort` (P02) — ҳоло воқеӣ.

---

### 10. Frontend

| Экран | Route | Тавсиф |
|---|---|---|
| Subscription | `/company/settings/subscription` | Ҳолат (badge бо ранг), план, санаи охир, истифода (progress bar-ҳо барои stores/users/products), таърихи пардохтҳо, «дархости иваз кардани план», toggle-и cancel-at-period-end |
| Баннери глобалӣ | company layout | GRACE: огоҳӣ (зард); SOFT_BLOCK: «Фармоишҳои нав қабул намешаванд» (норинҷӣ); FULL_BLOCK/CANCELLED: (сурх) + чӣ дастрас аст |
| Store огоҳӣ | store catalog (P07) | Агар company `NEW_ORDER`-ро манъ дошта бошад: «Ин таъминкунанда ҳоло фармоиш қабул намекунад» |
| Admin: subscriptions | `/admin/subscriptions` | Ҷадвал, filter; detail: history, payments, форми сабти пардохт (preview-и давраи нав пеш аз тасдиқ), change plan, extend trial |
| Admin: plans | `/admin/plans` | CRUD (бе delete) |
| Admin: plan requests | `/admin/plan-requests` | |

Frontend тугмаҳоро аз рӯи `allowed_actions` ғайрифаъол мекунад (FE-004).

---

### 11. Tests

```text
test_sub_002_trial_created_on_company_created
test_sub_004_period_calc_active_future_extends_from_end / expired_starts_now / month_end_edge
test_sub_005_tick_trial_to_grace / grace_to_soft / soft_to_full / active_to_cancelled
test_sub_005_tick_idempotent_double_run
test_sub_006_guard_matrix (parametrized: ҳар status × ҳар SubAction)
test_sub_007_no_data_deleted_on_full_block
test_sub_008_limit_users / limit_reached_details / null_means_unlimited
test_sub_009_downgrade_keeps_existing_blocks_new
test_sub_010_reminders_sent_once
test_sub_003_payment_amount_mismatch_requires_reason
test_subscription_payment_idempotent
test_permission_matrix_p03
```

### 12. Part Acceptance

```text
[ ] Company-и нав TRIAL мегирад
[ ] Job ҳамаи transition-ҳоро дуруст ва idempotent иҷро мекунад (тест бо freezegun)
[ ] SUPERADMIN пардохт сабт мекунад → ACTIVE, давра дуруст
[ ] Guard ва лимитҳо барои ҳар status тест шудаанд
[ ] UI: баннерҳо, саҳифаи subscription, admin
[ ] PART_REPORT.md
```

## [P04_catalog_pricing] P04 — CATALOG, PRICING & IMPORT

**Вобастагӣ:** P00–P03
**Мақсад:** категорияҳо, product-ҳо, воҳидҳо (base + sale units), price list-ҳо бо таърихи нарх, import аз Excel. Ҳама танҳо дар тарафи Company. Каталоги store дар P07.

---

### 1. Data Model

#### 1.1. `categories`

| Field | Type | Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| id | UUID | PK |
| company_id | UUID | FK companies |
| parent_id | UUID | NULL FK categories (ҳамон company; чуқурӣ ≤ 2) |
| name | VARCHAR(120) | NOT NULL |
| sort_order | INTEGER | DEFAULT 0 |
| is_active | BOOLEAN | DEFAULT true |
| created_at / updated_at / version | | |

UNIQUE (`company_id`, `parent_id`, lower(`name`)) (NULLS NOT DISTINCT).

#### 1.2. `products`

| Field | Type | Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| id | UUID | PK |
| company_id | UUID | FK |
| category_id | UUID | NULL FK categories (ҳамон company — trigger) |
| sku | VARCHAR(64) | NOT NULL; `^[A-Za-z0-9._-]+$` |
| name | VARCHAR(255) | NOT NULL |
| description | TEXT | NULL |
| barcode | VARCHAR(32) | NULL |
| base_unit | VARCHAR(8) | CHECK IN (`PCS`,`KG`,`G`,`L`,`ML`,`M`,`PACK`) |
| image_file_id | UUID | NULL FK stored_files |
| is_active | BOOLEAN | DEFAULT true |
| created_by | UUID | FK users |
| created_at / updated_at / version | | |

Constraints: UNIQUE (`company_id`, `sku`) — `uq_products_company_sku`; UNIQUE (`company_id`, `barcode`) WHERE barcode IS NOT NULL — `uq_products_company_barcode`. Index: `pg_trgm` GIN дар `name`, `sku`.

#### 1.3. `product_units`

| Field | Type | Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| id | UUID | PK |
| product_id | UUID | FK products |
| code | VARCHAR(16) | NOT NULL (масалан `PCS`, `BOX24`) |
| name | JSONB | `{"tg","ru","en"}` |
| coefficient | NUMERIC(14,3) | CHECK > 0 — чанд base unit дар як sale unit |
| is_base | BOOLEAN | NOT NULL |
| allow_fraction | BOOLEAN | DEFAULT false (true барои KG, L) |
| min_order_qty | NUMERIC(14,3) | DEFAULT 1, CHECK > 0 |
| is_active | BOOLEAN | DEFAULT true |
| created_at | timestamptz | |

Constraints: UNIQUE (`product_id`, `code`); partial UNIQUE (`product_id`) WHERE `is_base`; CHECK (`NOT is_base OR coefficient = 1`). `coefficient` ва `code` **immutable** (trigger). Барои тағйир — unit-ро deactivate кун ва нав соз.

#### 1.4. `price_lists`

| Field | Type | Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| id | UUID | PK |
| company_id | UUID | FK |
| code | VARCHAR(32) | UNIQUE (`company_id`, `code`) |
| name | VARCHAR(120) | NOT NULL |
| is_default | BOOLEAN | partial UNIQUE (`company_id`) WHERE `is_default` |
| is_active | BOOLEAN | DEFAULT true |
| created_at / updated_at / version | | |

Price list-и default (`DEFAULT`) автоматӣ ҳангоми аввалин дастрасӣ ба pricing ё handler-и `COMPANY_CREATED` сохта мешавад. Default-ро deactivate кардан манъ (`default_price_list_required`).

#### 1.5. `prices`

| Field | Type | Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| id | UUID | PK |
| price_list_id | UUID | FK |
| product_unit_id | UUID | FK |
| price | NUMERIC(12,2) | CHECK > 0 |
| valid_from | timestamptz | NOT NULL |
| valid_to | timestamptz | NULL (кушода) |
| created_by | UUID | FK users |
| created_at | timestamptz | |

- `EXCLUDE USING gist (price_list_id WITH =, product_unit_id WITH =, tstzrange(valid_from, valid_to, '[)') WITH &&)` (extension `btree_gist`) — `price_overlap`.
- Trigger: UPDATE танҳо `valid_to` аз NULL ба қимат иҷозат; DELETE танҳо агар `valid_from > now()` (нархи ҳанӯз фаъолнашуда).

---

### 2. Business rules

| ID | Қоида |
|---|---|
| CAT-001 | Ҳамаи query-ҳо бо `company_id = active org` филтр мешаванд; product-и company-и дигар → 404. |
| CAT-002 | Сохтани product дар як transaction: `products` + `product_units(is_base, code=base_unit, coefficient=1, allow_fraction = base_unit IN (KG,G,L,ML,M))`. |
| CAT-003 | `base_unit` пас аз аввалин stock movement ё order item immutable (`base_unit_immutable`). |
| CAT-004 | Product-и `is_active=false` ба каталоги store ва ба cart намеояд; order-ҳои мавҷуда snapshot доранд (P07). |
| CAT-005 | Лимити `products` (SUB-008) ҳисоби product-ҳои `is_active=true`; санҷиш ҳангоми create, activate, import. |
| CAT-006 | Ҳамаи write-ҳо: `SubscriptionGuard.require(CATALOG_WRITE)`. |
| CAT-007 | Категорияи дорои product ё зеркатегорияи фаъол deactivate намешавад (`category_not_empty`). |
| CAT-008 | Unit-и base deactivate намешавад. |
| PRC-001 | Гузоштани нарх: `PriceService.set_price(list, unit, price, valid_from=now|future)`: сатри кушодаи ҷориро `valid_to = valid_from` мекунад ва сатри нав илова мекунад — дар як transaction бо `FOR UPDATE` бар сатри кушода. |
| PRC-002 | Нархи оянда (`valid_from > now`) иҷозат; танҳо як нархи ояндаи кушода барои як (list, unit); бекор кардани он — DELETE (audit `price.cancelled`) ва `valid_to`-и сатри қаблӣ ба NULL барқарор мешавад. |
| PRC-003 | `PriceService.resolve(price_list_id, product_unit_id, at) -> Decimal \| None`: аввал price list-и додашуда; агар нарх нест → default list; агар нест → `None` (product барои ин партнёр фармоишнашаванда) — DEC-04. |
| PRC-004 | Нарх барои ҳар sale unit алоҳида муайян мешавад (нархи box аз нархи pcs автоматӣ ҳисоб **намешавад**). |
| PRC-005 | Таърихи нарх ҳеҷ гоҳ тағйир намеёбад (ба ҷуз бастани `valid_to`). |
| IMP-001 | Lifecycle-и import — §3. Ҳадди аксар 5000 сатр, файл ≤ 5 MB, танҳо `.xlsx`. |
| IMP-002 | Import **ҳама-ё-ҳеҷ**: агар ҳатто як сатр хато дошта бошад, `CONFIRM` манъ (`import_has_errors`). Import дар як DB transaction. |
| IMP-003 | Шаблонҳо (`GET /imports/templates/{kind}`) бо сарлавҳаҳо дар забони user ва варақи «Дастур». |
| IMP-004 | Products import: upsert аз рӯи `sku`. Сатри мавҷуда: `name, category, barcode, description, is_active` навсозӣ; `base_unit` агар фарқ кунад → хатои сатр `base_unit_immutable`. Категорияи номаълум автоматӣ сохта мешавад (дар preview нишон дода мешавад). |
| IMP-005 | Prices import: `price_list_code, sku, unit_code, price, valid_from?` — ҳар сатр тавассути `PriceService.set_price`. |
| IMP-006 | Натиҷа: `{created, updated, skipped}` ва ҳисобот дар UI; файли аслӣ ҳамчун `stored_files(IMPORT)` нигоҳ дошта мешавад. |

---

### 3. Import lifecycle

| From | To | Actor | Тавсиф |
|---|---|---|---|
| — | UPLOADED | OWNER/MANAGER | Файл қабул шуд; task-и `parse_import` навбат гузошта шуд |
| UPLOADED | VALIDATED | System | Parse + validate; сатрҳо дар `import_rows`, хатоҳо дар `import_errors` |
| UPLOADED | FAILED | System | Файл вайрон (`import_file_invalid`) |
| VALIDATED | CONFIRMED | OWNER/MANAGER | `error_count = 0`; Idempotency-Key |
| CONFIRMED | COMPLETED | System | Task-и `run_import` дар як transaction |
| CONFIRMED | FAILED | System | Exception → rollback; `failure_reason` |
| UPLOADED, VALIDATED | CANCELLED | OWNER/MANAGER | |

#### 3.1. Ҷадвалҳои import

`imports`: `id, company_id, kind (PRODUCTS|PRICES|STOCK), status, file_id, total_rows, error_count, summary JSONB, failure_reason, created_by, confirmed_by, created_at, updated_at, version`.
(`STOCK` дар P05 истифода мешавад.)

`import_rows`: `id, import_id, row_number, data JSONB, action (CREATE|UPDATE|SKIP)`.

`import_errors`: `id, import_id, row_number, field, error_code, message_key, params JSONB`.

| `import.run` | ✓ | ✓ | — | — | — |

Store role-ҳо ба ин endpoint-ҳо дастрасӣ надоранд (каталоги store — P07).

## [P05_inventory] P05 — INVENTORY

**Вобастагӣ:** P00–P04
**Мақсад:** stock дар base unit, журнали append-only-и ҳаракатҳо, reservation-и atomic бо row locking, амалҳои анбор (қабул, ислоҳ, партофтан), сигнали low stock.

Як анбор барои ҳар company (DEC-25).

---

### 1. Data Model

#### 1.1. `stocks` (projection)

| Field | Type | Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| product_id | UUID | PK, FK products |
| company_id | UUID | FK, INDEX |
| quantity | NUMERIC(14,3) | NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, CHECK ≥ 0 |
| reserved_quantity | NUMERIC(14,3) | NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, CHECK ≥ 0 |
| low_stock_threshold | NUMERIC(14,3) | NULL, CHECK ≥ 0 |
| last_movement_at | timestamptz | NULL |
| updated_at / version | | |

CHECK `reserved_quantity <= quantity` — `ck_stocks_reserved_le_quantity`.
`available = quantity − reserved_quantity` (дар DB ҳисоб намешавад; дар query ҳамчун expression).

Сатри `stocks` ҳангоми `PRODUCT_CREATED` (handler) сохта мешавад; migration барои product-ҳои мавҷуда backfill мекунад.

`stocks` **танҳо** тавассути `StockService` тағйир меёбад, ҳамеша ҳамроҳи сатри `stock_movements` дар ҳамон transaction.

#### 1.2. `stock_movements` (append-only)

| Field | Type | Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| id | UUID | PK |
| company_id | UUID | FK |
| product_id | UUID | FK |
| type | VARCHAR(16) | CHECK IN (`RECEIPT`,`ADJUSTMENT`,`WRITE_OFF`,`RESERVE`,`RELEASE`,`SHIP`,`RETURN_IN`) |
| quantity_delta | NUMERIC(14,3) | NOT NULL |
| reserved_delta | NUMERIC(14,3) | NOT NULL |
| quantity_after | NUMERIC(14,3) | NOT NULL |
| reserved_after | NUMERIC(14,3) | NOT NULL |
| source_type | VARCHAR(16) | CHECK IN (`MANUAL`,`IMPORT`,`ORDER`,`RETURN`) |
| source_id | UUID | NULL |
| reason | TEXT | NULL (ҳатмӣ барои ADJUSTMENT, WRITE_OFF) |
| created_by | UUID | NULL (system) |
| created_at | timestamptz | |

Index: (`product_id`, `created_at DESC`), (`company_id`, `created_at DESC`), (`source_type`, `source_id`). Trigger `forbid_mutation` (UPDATE/DELETE).

Таъсири ҳар навъ:

| Type | quantity_delta | reserved_delta |
|---|---|---|
| RECEIPT | +q | 0 |
| ADJUSTMENT | ±q (actual − current) | 0 |
| WRITE_OFF | −q | 0 |
| RESERVE | 0 | +q |
| RELEASE | 0 | −q |
| SHIP | −q | −q |
| RETURN_IN | +q | 0 |

#### 1.3. `stock_reservations`

| Field | Type | Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| id | UUID | PK |
| company_id | UUID | FK |
| product_id | UUID | FK |
| source_type | VARCHAR(16) | `ORDER` |
| source_id | UUID | NOT NULL |
| quantity | NUMERIC(14,3) | CHECK > 0 |
| status | VARCHAR(16) | CHECK IN (`ACTIVE`,`RELEASED`,`SHIPPED`) |
| created_at / closed_at | timestamptz | |

Partial UNIQUE (`source_type`, `source_id`, `product_id`) WHERE `status = 'ACTIVE'`.

---

### 2. StockService (internal contract — P07, P08, P10 истифода мебаранд)

```python
class StockService:
    async def availability(company_id, product_ids) -> dict[UUID, Availability]   # бе lock
    async def reserve(company_id, lines: list[(product_id, qty_base)], source: SourceRef, actor) -> None
    async def release(source: SourceRef, actor, reason) -> None       # ҳамаи ACTIVE → RELEASED
    async def ship(source: SourceRef, actor) -> None                  # ҳамаи ACTIVE → SHIPPED
    async def return_in(company_id, lines, source: SourceRef, actor) -> None
    async def receive(company_id, lines, actor, note) -> None
    async def adjust(company_id, product_id, actual_qty, reason, actor) -> None
    async def write_off(company_id, product_id, qty, reason, actor) -> None
```

---

### 3. Business rules

| ID | Қоида |
|---|---|
| INV-001 | Ҳама миқдорҳо дар **base unit** (DEC-03). API-и амалҳои дастӣ `unit_id` қабул мекунад ва ба base табдил медиҳад: `qty_base = q3(qty × coefficient)`. |
| INV-002 | `reserve` **atomic ва ҳама-ё-ҳеҷ**: сатрҳои `stocks` бо `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` **бо тартиби `product_id`** (пешгирии deadlock) қуфл мешаванд; агар ягон сатр `available < qty` → `409 insufficient_stock` бо рӯйхати **ҳамаи** сатрҳои нокифоя; ҳеҷ чиз тағйир намеёбад. |
| INV-003 | Stock-и манфӣ манъ аст (DEC; танзимшаванда нест дар MVP). Ҳар кӯшиш → `negative_stock_forbidden`; CHECK-и DB ҳимояи охирин. |
| INV-004 | `release` ва `ship` танҳо reservation-ҳои `ACTIVE`-и ҳамон source-ро истифода мебаранд; такрор (reservation нест) → no-op (idempotent). |
| INV-005 | `adjust`: `actual_qty ≥ reserved_quantity`, вагарна `negative_stock_forbidden` бо `details.reserved`. `reason` ҳатмӣ (≥ 5 аломат). |
| INV-006 | `write_off`: `qty ≤ available`; `reason` ҳатмӣ. |
| INV-007 | Low stock: пас аз ҳар movement, агар `available < low_stock_threshold` ва дар 24 соати охир event барои ин product набуд → event `LOW_STOCK`. |
| INV-008 | Амалҳои дастӣ: `SubscriptionGuard.require(CATALOG_WRITE)`. Reserve/ship аз P07/P08 guard-и худро доранд. |
| INV-009 | Reconciliation (Celery, ҳаррӯза 03:00 UTC+5): `stocks.quantity = Σ quantity_delta` ва `reserved_quantity = Σ reserved_delta = Σ ACTIVE reservations`. Мувофиқ набошад → event `STOCK_RECONCILIATION_MISMATCH` (→ SUPERADMIN + Sentry), **ислоҳи автоматӣ нест**. |
| INV-010 | Import-и stock (`imports.kind = STOCK`): сутунҳо `sku, unit_code, quantity, note?` → ҳар сатр `RECEIPT`; ҳама-ё-ҳеҷ (IMP-002). |

---

### 4. Permission matrix (P05)

| Permission | C.OWNER | C.MANAGER | C.OPERATOR | C.WAREHOUSE | C.COURIER |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `stock.view` | ✓ | ✓ | ✓ | ✓ | — |
| `stock.receive` | ✓ | ✓ | — | ✓ | — |
| `stock.adjust` | ✓ | ✓ | — | — | — |
| `stock.write_off` | ✓ | ✓ | — | — | — |
| `stock.settings` (threshold) | ✓ | ✓ | — | ✓ | — |

### 5. API

| Method | Path | Permission | Idempotent | Тавсиф |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/v1/inventory/stocks` | view | — | `product, quantity, reserved, available, threshold`; filter `category_id`, `low_stock=true`, `is_active`; search `name, sku, barcode`; ordering `available`, `name` |
| GET | `/api/v1/inventory/stocks/{product_id}` | view | — | + reservation-ҳои ACTIVE (бо рақами order) |
| PATCH | `/api/v1/inventory/stocks/{product_id}` | settings | — | `{low_stock_threshold, version}` |
| POST | `/api/v1/inventory/receipts` | receive | ✓ | `{items:[{product_id, unit_id, quantity}], note}` — ≤ 200 сатр, як transaction |
| POST | `/api/v1/inventory/adjustments` | adjust | ✓ | `{product_id, actual_quantity (base), reason}` |
| POST | `/api/v1/inventory/write-offs` | write_off | ✓ | `{product_id, unit_id, quantity, reason}` |
| GET | `/api/v1/inventory/movements` | view | — | filter `product_id`, `type`, `source_type`, `date_from`, `date_to`; ordering `-created_at` |

Audit: `stock.received`, `stock.adjusted` (old/new), `stock.written_off`, `stock.threshold_changed`.
Events: `LOW_STOCK`, `STOCK_RECONCILIATION_MISMATCH`.

| Экран | Route | Тавсиф |

| Stock | `/company/warehouse/stock` | Ҷадвал: product, мавҷуд, захира, дастрас (ранги сурх агар < threshold); filter «кам»; тугмаҳо мувофиқи permission |
| Stock detail | `/company/warehouse/stock/:productId` | Рақамҳо, reservation-ҳо (линк ба order), журнали movements, таҳрири threshold |
| Қабули мол | `/company/warehouse/receipts/new` | Ҷустуҷӯи product (barcode scanner-и клавиатуравӣ ҳам), интихоби unit, миқдор; preview «+24 BOX24 = +576 PCS» → тасдиқ |
| Инвентаризатсия | dialog дар detail | Ворид кардани миқдори воқеӣ → preview-и фарқ (±) → reason → тасдиқ |
| Партофтан | dialog | миқдор, reason |
| Movements | `/company/warehouse/movements` | Журнал бо filter |

## [P06_partnerships] P06 — PARTNERSHIPS & TERMS

**Вобастагӣ:** P00–P05
**Мақсад:** муносибати Company ↔ Store, даъват аз ду тараф, terms-и versioned ва immutable, лимити `active_stores`.

---

### 1. Data Model

#### 1.1. `partnerships`

| Field | Type | Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| id | UUID | PK |
| company_id | UUID | FK companies |
| store_id | UUID | FK stores |
| status | VARCHAR(16) | CHECK IN (`PENDING`,`ACTIVE`,`SUSPENDED`,`TERMINATED`,`DECLINED`,`CANCELLED`) |
| initiated_by_side | VARCHAR(8) | CHECK IN (`COMPANY`,`STORE`) |
| initiated_by | UUID | FK users |
| customer_code | VARCHAR(32) | NULL — рамзи дохилии company барои store; UNIQUE (`company_id`, `customer_code`) WHERE NOT NULL |
| activated_at / suspended_at / ended_at | timestamptz | NULL |
| end_reason | TEXT | NULL |
| created_at / updated_at / version | | |

Partial UNIQUE (`company_id`, `store_id`) WHERE `status IN ('PENDING','ACTIVE','SUSPENDED')` — `uq_partnership_open` (PRT-002).
Index: (`company_id`, `status`), (`store_id`, `status`).

#### 1.2. `partnership_terms` (append-only)

| Field | Type | Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| id | UUID | PK |
| partnership_id | UUID | FK |
| version_no | INTEGER | UNIQUE (`partnership_id`, `version_no`), CHECK ≥ 1 |
| price_list_id | UUID | FK price_lists (ҳамон company) |
| credit_limit | NUMERIC(12,2) | NOT NULL, CHECK ≥ 0 |
| credit_days | SMALLINT | CHECK BETWEEN 0 AND 180 |
| payment_methods | VARCHAR(16)[] | NOT NULL, зерҷамъи {`CASH`,`BANK_TRANSFER`}, холӣ не |
| minimum_order_amount | NUMERIC(12,2) | CHECK ≥ 0 |
| delivery_fee | NUMERIC(12,2) | CHECK ≥ 0 |
| free_delivery_threshold | NUMERIC(12,2) | NULL, CHECK > 0 |
| return_days | SMALLINT | CHECK BETWEEN 0 AND 60, DEFAULT 14 |
| dispute_window_hours | SMALLINT | CHECK BETWEEN 1 AND 168, DEFAULT 48 |
| effective_from | timestamptz | NOT NULL |
| note | TEXT | NULL |
| created_by | UUID | FK users |
| created_at | timestamptz | |

Trigger `forbid_mutation`. `effective_until` нигоҳ дошта намешавад — он `effective_from`-и версияи навбатӣ аст.

`TermsService.current(partnership_id, at=now)` → версия бо `max(effective_from) ≤ at`.

---

### 2. State machine

| From | To | Actor | Шартҳо | Side effects |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| — | PENDING | C.OWNER/MANAGER (invite) | Store ёфт шуд (PRT-004); terms v1 дода шуд; `PARTNERSHIP_NEW` | Terms v1; event `PARTNERSHIP_INVITED` |
| — | PENDING | S.OWNER (request) | `public_code` дуруст | Бе terms; event `PARTNERSHIP_REQUESTED` |
| PENDING | ACTIVE | Тарафи **гиранда** (S.OWNER агар company даъват карда бошад; C.OWNER/MANAGER агар store) | Ҳарду `APPROVED` (DEC-19); terms мавҷуд (агар store request карда бошад — terms v1 дар ҳамин амал); `PARTNERSHIP_NEW`; лимити `active_stores` | `activated_at`; event `PARTNERSHIP_ACTIVATED` |
| PENDING | DECLINED | Тарафи гиранда | `reason?` | event |
| PENDING | CANCELLED | Тарафи даъваткунанда | — | |
| ACTIVE | SUSPENDED | C.OWNER/MANAGER | `reason` | Order-и нав манъ; event |
| SUSPENDED | ACTIVE | C.OWNER/MANAGER | Ҳарду `APPROVED`; subscription ≠ FULL_BLOCK/CANCELLED | event |
| ACTIVE, SUSPENDED | TERMINATED | C.OWNER ё S.OWNER | `reason` | PRT-009; event `PARTNERSHIP_TERMINATED` |

TERMINATED, DECLINED, CANCELLED — ниҳоӣ. Барои ҳамкории нав partnership-и **нав** сохта мешавад (таърихи кӯҳна боқӣ).

---

### 3. Business rules

| ID | Қоида |
|---|---|
| PRT-001 | Ҳар order, payment, ledger entry ба `partnership_id` пайваст аст (P07, P09). |
| PRT-002 | Барои як (company, store) танҳо як partnership-и кушода — constraint + санҷиш дар service (`partnership_exists`). |
| PRT-003 | Activation: `organization_not_verified` бо `details.side`, агар ягон тараф `APPROVED` набошад. |
| PRT-004 | Ҷустуҷӯи store барои даъват танҳо бо **мувофиқати пурра**-и `phone` ё `tax_identifier` → `{id, name, city}`; рӯйхати умумии store-ҳо дастрас нест. |
| PRT-005 | Лимити `active_stores` = шумораи partnership-ҳои `ACTIVE` + `SUSPENDED`; санҷиш ҳангоми `→ ACTIVE` (SUB-008). |
| PRT-006 | Terms-и нав: `version_no = max + 1`; `effective_from ≥ now − 1 дақиқа` ва `> effective_from`-и версияи охирин (`terms_effective_from_invalid`). Terms-и оянда иҷозат. |
| PRT-007 | Тағйири `credit_limit` ё `credit_days` нисбат ба версияи ҷорӣ — танҳо C.OWNER (`terms.manage_credit`). Field-ҳои дигар — OWNER/MANAGER. |
| PRT-008 | `price_list_id` бояд ба ҳамон company тааллуқ дошта ва `is_active` бошад. |
| PRT-009 | TERMINATED: order-ҳои `NEW`/`VIEWED` автоматӣ `CANCELLED` (reason `PARTNERSHIP_TERMINATED`) тавассути `OrderService` (Port — P07); order-ҳои тасдиқшуда то охир мераванд; payment, return, dispute иҷозат; ledger барои ҳарду тараф хондашаванда боқӣ мемонад. |
| PRT-010 | SUSPENDED: order-и нав манъ (`partnership_not_active`); order-ҳои мавҷуда идома; payment иҷозат. |
| PRT-011 | Store маълумоти company-ро танҳо дар доираи partnership мебинад: `name, phone, city, address, verification_status`. Company маълумоти store-ро: ҳамаи field-ҳои профил ба ҷуз `tax_identifier` агар `APPROVED` набошад. |
| PRT-012 | Store метавонад бо чанд company partnership дошта бошад; ҳар partnership terms ва ledger-и алоҳида дорад. |

---

### 4. Permission matrix (P06)

| Permission | C.OWNER | C.MANAGER | C.OPERATOR | C.WAREHOUSE | C.COURIER | S.OWNER | S.SELLER |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `partners.view` | ✓ | ✓ | ✓ | — | — | ✓ | ✓ |
| `partners.manage` (invite, accept, decline, suspend) | ✓ | ✓ | — | — | — | ✓ | — |
| `partners.terminate` | ✓ | — | — | — | — | ✓ | — |
| `terms.view` | ✓ | ✓ | ✓ | — | — | ✓ | ✓ |
| `terms.manage` | ✓ | ✓ | — | — | — | — | — |
| `terms.manage_credit` | ✓ | — | — | — | — | — | — |

---

### 5. API

| Method | Path | Permission | Idempotent | Тавсиф |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/v1/partnerships` | view | — | Барои company: store-ҳо; барои store: company-ҳо. filter `status`; search номи тарафи дигар, `customer_code` |
| GET | `/api/v1/partnerships/{id}` | view | — | + terms-и ҷорӣ ва оянда |
| GET | `/api/v1/partnerships/store-lookup` | manage (company) | — | `?phone=` ё `?tax_identifier=` |
| POST | `/api/v1/partnerships/invite` | manage (company) | ✓ | `{store_id, customer_code?, terms:{…}}` |
| POST | `/api/v1/partnerships/request` | manage (store) | ✓ | `{company_public_code}` |
| POST | `/api/v1/partnerships/{id}/accept` | manage | ✓ | Company: `{terms}` ҳатмӣ агар terms нест |
| POST | `/api/v1/partnerships/{id}/decline` | manage | — | `{reason?}` |
| POST | `/api/v1/partnerships/{id}/cancel` | manage | — | |
| POST | `/api/v1/partnerships/{id}/suspend` | manage (company) | — | `{reason}` |
| POST | `/api/v1/partnerships/{id}/reactivate` | manage (company) | — | |
| POST | `/api/v1/partnerships/{id}/terminate` | terminate | — | `{reason}` |
| PATCH | `/api/v1/partnerships/{id}` | manage (company) | — | `{customer_code, version}` |
| GET | `/api/v1/partnerships/{id}/terms` | terms.view | — | Ҳамаи версияҳо |
| POST | `/api/v1/partnerships/{id}/terms` | terms.manage | ✓ | Версияи нав |

---

### 6. Audit ва events

Audit: `partnership.invited`, `.requested`, `.activated`, `.declined`, `.cancelled`, `.suspended`, `.reactivated`, `.terminated`, `terms.created` (бо diff нисбат ба версияи қаблӣ).
Events: `PARTNERSHIP_INVITED`, `PARTNERSHIP_REQUESTED`, `PARTNERSHIP_ACTIVATED`, `PARTNERSHIP_SUSPENDED`, `PARTNERSHIP_TERMINATED`, `TERMS_CHANGED` (→ P11 ба ҳарду тараф).

### 7. Ports

| Port | Default дар P06 | Implementation |
|---|---|---|
| `OrderCancellationPort.cancel_open_orders(partnership_id, reason)` | no-op | P07 |

---

### 8. Frontend

#### Company area

| Экран | Route | Тавсиф |
|---|---|---|
| Мизоҷон | `/company/partners` | Tabs аз рӯи status; ҷадвал: store, шаҳр, customer_code, лимити кредит, ҳолат; badge-и дархостҳои нав |
| Даъват | dialog | 1) ҷустуҷӯ бо phone/ИНН 2) натиҷа 3) форми terms (price list, кредит, рӯзҳо, усулҳои пардохт, ҳадди ақали order, delivery fee, return/dispute) 4) preview → тасдиқ |
| Partner detail | `/company/partners/:id` | Профили store (харита нест; тугмаи «Кушодан дар харита» — линки берунӣ бо координата), terms-и ҷорӣ, terms-и оянда, таърихи terms бо diff (сабз/сурх), амалҳо (suspend/reactivate/terminate бо reason ва тасдиқи номи store) |
| Terms-и нав | dialog | Формаи пуршуда бо версияи ҷорӣ; field-ҳои кредит барои MANAGER readonly; `effective_from` (default: ҳозир) |

#### Store area

| Экран | Route | Тавсиф |
|---|---|---|
| Таъминкунандагон | `/store/suppliers` | Рӯйхат, ҳолат |
| Дархост | dialog | Ворид кардани рамзи 8-ҳарфаи company |
| Даъватҳо | `/store/suppliers?tab=invitations` | Preview-и terms → accept/decline |
| Supplier detail | `/store/suppliers/:id` | Маълумоти company, terms-и ҷорӣ (хондан), terminate (OWNER) |

## [P07_orders] P07 — ORDERS

**Вобастагӣ:** P00–P06
**Мақсад:** каталоги store, cart, сохтани order (аз store ё аз номи store аз ҷониби company), state machine-и пурра, confirmation бо stock/credit/minimum, snapshot, cancellation, completion.

---

### 1. Data Model

#### 1.1. `carts` ва `cart_items`

`carts`: `id, partnership_id, user_id, updated_at` — UNIQUE (`partnership_id`, `user_id`).
`cart_items`: `id, cart_id (FK CASCADE), product_unit_id, quantity NUMERIC(14,3) CHECK > 0, added_at` — UNIQUE (`cart_id`, `product_unit_id`).

Cart нарх нигоҳ намедорад — нарх ҳар дафъа ҳисоб мешавад (PRC-003).

#### 1.2. `orders`

| Field | Type | Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| id | UUID | PK |
| company_id | UUID | FK (denormalized барои scope/index) |
| store_id | UUID | FK (denormalized) |
| partnership_id | UUID | FK |
| order_number | VARCHAR(20) | UNIQUE (`company_id`, `order_number`); формат `ORD-2026-000123` (sequence: `order_number`, key `<company_id>:<year>`) |
| source | VARCHAR(16) | CHECK IN (`STORE`,`COMPANY_ON_BEHALF`) |
| status | VARCHAR(24) | CHECK IN (§2) |
| currency | CHAR(3) | `TJS` |
| requested_subtotal | NUMERIC(12,2) | CHECK ≥ 0 |
| subtotal | NUMERIC(12,2) | NULL то confirmation; CHECK ≥ 0 |
| discount | NUMERIC(12,2) | DEFAULT 0; CHECK ≥ 0 AND (subtotal IS NULL OR discount ≤ subtotal) |
| discount_reason | TEXT | NULL; ҳатмӣ агар discount > 0 |
| delivery_fee | NUMERIC(12,2) | DEFAULT 0, CHECK ≥ 0 |
| total | NUMERIC(12,2) | NULL то confirmation; CHECK total = subtotal − discount + delivery_fee |
| terms_id | UUID | NULL FK partnership_terms — версия дар confirmation |
| terms_snapshot | JSONB | NULL — нусхаи пурраи terms дар confirmation |
| delivery_address | VARCHAR(500) | NOT NULL — snapshot аз store ҳангоми сохтан |
| delivery_latitude / delivery_longitude | NUMERIC(9,6) | NULL — snapshot |
| store_note / company_note | TEXT | NULL |
| created_by | UUID | FK users |
| confirmed_by / confirmed_at | | NULL |
| delivered_at / completed_at / cancelled_at | timestamptz | NULL |
| cancel_reason / rejection_reason | TEXT | NULL |
| credit_override_reason / minimum_override_reason | TEXT | NULL |
| created_at / updated_at / version | | |

Index: (`company_id`, `status`, `created_at DESC`), (`store_id`, `created_at DESC`), (`partnership_id`, `status`), GIN trgm (`order_number`).

#### 1.3. `order_items`

| Field | Type | Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| id | UUID | PK |
| order_id | UUID | FK |
| product_id | UUID | FK (барои ишора; маълумот аз snapshot) |
| product_unit_id | UUID | FK |
| product_name_snapshot | VARCHAR(255) | NOT NULL |
| sku_snapshot | VARCHAR(64) | NOT NULL |
| unit_code_snapshot | VARCHAR(16) | NOT NULL |
| unit_name_snapshot | JSONB | NOT NULL |
| unit_coefficient_snapshot | NUMERIC(14,3) | NOT NULL |
| allow_fraction_snapshot | BOOLEAN | NOT NULL |
| base_unit_snapshot | VARCHAR(8) | NOT NULL |
| requested_quantity | NUMERIC(14,3) | CHECK > 0 |
| confirmed_quantity | NUMERIC(14,3) | NULL; CHECK 0 ≤ confirmed ≤ requested |
| unit_price | NUMERIC(12,2) | CHECK > 0 — нарх дар вақти **сохтани** order |
| line_total | NUMERIC(12,2) | = `q2(coalesce(confirmed, requested) × unit_price)` |

UNIQUE (`order_id`, `product_unit_id`). Field-ҳои `*_snapshot`, `requested_quantity`, `unit_price` immutable (trigger); `confirmed_quantity` танҳо як бор аз NULL (trigger).
`rejected_quantity` = `requested − confirmed` (дар schema-и API ҳисоб мешавад).

#### 1.4. `order_status_history` (append-only)

`id, order_id, from_status NULL, to_status, actor_id NULL, actor_type (USER|SYSTEM), reason, metadata JSONB, created_at`. Index (`order_id`, `created_at`).

---

### 2. State machine (ORD-010)

| # | From | To | Actor | Шартҳо | Side effects | Event |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| T1 | — | NEW | S.OWNER/SELLER ё C.OWNER/MANAGER/OPERATOR (on behalf) | ORD-001..005 | snapshot-и product/unit/нарх/address | `ORDER_CREATED` |
| T2 | NEW | VIEWED | Company user (`orders.view`) | Аввалин кушодан | — | — |
| T3 | NEW, VIEWED | CONFIRMED | C.OWNER/MANAGER/OPERATOR | ORD-020 | reserve stock, terms snapshot, totals | `ORDER_CONFIRMED` |
| T4 | NEW, VIEWED | PARTIALLY_CONFIRMED | ҳамон | ORD-020; ақаллан як `confirmed < requested` ва ақаллан як `confirmed > 0` | ҳамон | `ORDER_CONFIRMED` (`partial=true`) |
| T5 | NEW, VIEWED | REJECTED | C.OWNER/MANAGER/OPERATOR | `reason` | — | `ORDER_REJECTED` |
| T6 | NEW, VIEWED | CANCELLED | Store (ORD-030) ё C.OWNER/MANAGER | ORD-030 | — | `ORDER_CANCELLED` |
| T7 | CONFIRMED, PARTIALLY_CONFIRMED | ASSEMBLING | C.OWNER/MANAGER/WAREHOUSE | `ORDER_FULFILLMENT` | — | `ORDER_ASSEMBLING` |
| T8 | ASSEMBLING | READY_FOR_DELIVERY | C.OWNER/MANAGER/WAREHOUSE | ҳамон | — | `ORDER_READY` (→ P08: Delivery PLANNED) |
| T9 | CONFIRMED, PARTIALLY_CONFIRMED, ASSEMBLING, READY_FOR_DELIVERY | CANCELLED | C.OWNER/MANAGER | `reason`; delivery-и фаъол нест ё бекор мешавад (P08) | `StockService.release` | `ORDER_CANCELLED` |
| T10 | READY_FOR_DELIVERY | IN_TRANSIT | **SYSTEM** (DeliveryService) | — | — | `ORDER_DISPATCHED` |
| T11 | IN_TRANSIT | DELIVERED | **SYSTEM** (DeliveryService) | — | `StockService.ship`; `delivered_at` | `ORDER_DELIVERED` (→ P09: charge) |
| T12 | IN_TRANSIT | DELIVERY_FAILED | **SYSTEM** (DeliveryService) | `reason` | reservation боқӣ | `ORDER_DELIVERY_FAILED` |
| T13 | DELIVERY_FAILED | READY_FOR_DELIVERY | C.OWNER/MANAGER | `ORDER_FULFILLMENT` | — | `ORDER_READY` |
| T14 | DELIVERY_FAILED | CANCELLED | C.OWNER/MANAGER | `reason` | `StockService.release` | `ORDER_CANCELLED` |
| T15 | DELIVERED | DISPUTED | **SYSTEM** (DisputeService, P10) | — | — | `ORDER_DISPUTED` |
| T16 | DELIVERED | COMPLETED | **SYSTEM** (job) | DEC-14 | `completed_at` | `ORDER_COMPLETED` |
| T17 | DISPUTED | COMPLETED | **SYSTEM** (DisputeService) | Dispute ҳал шуд | `completed_at` | `ORDER_COMPLETED` |
| T18 | NEW, VIEWED | CANCELLED | **SYSTEM** | PRT-009 (partnership terminated) | — | `ORDER_CANCELLED` |

Ҳолатҳои ниҳоӣ: `REJECTED`, `CANCELLED`, `COMPLETED`. Ҳар transition-и дигар → `409 invalid_transition`.

Transition-ҳои **SYSTEM** endpoint-и public надоранд; танҳо методҳои `OrderService` бо `SystemActor`, ки аз module-ҳои мувофиқ даъват мешаванд.

#### 2.1. OrderService — ягона дарвоза (ORD-011)

```python
class OrderService:
    async def create(ctx, partnership_id, items, note, source) -> Order
    async def mark_viewed(ctx, order_id) -> Order
    async def confirm(ctx, order_id, lines: dict[item_id, qty], discount, discount_reason,
                      override_credit_reason, override_minimum_reason, company_note, version) -> Order
    async def reject(ctx, order_id, reason, version) -> Order
    async def cancel(ctx, order_id, reason, version) -> Order
    async def start_assembling(ctx, order_id, version) -> Order
    async def mark_ready(ctx, order_id, version) -> Order
    async def reattempt(ctx, order_id, version) -> Order
    # SYSTEM:
    async def dispatch(system, order_id) / deliver(system, order_id, delivered_at)
    async def fail_delivery(system, order_id, reason) / mark_disputed(system, order_id)
    async def complete(system, order_id) / cancel_open_for_partnership(system, partnership_id, reason)
```

Ҳар метод: `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` бар order → санҷиши `version` (барои амалҳои user) → санҷиши transition аз ҷадвали §2 → иҷро → `order_status_history` → audit `order.transition` → event. `order.status = …` берун аз `_apply_transition` манъ (тест бо grep дар CI).

---

### 3. Business rules

#### 3.1. Сохтан

| ID | Қоида |
|---|---|
| ORD-001 | Partnership `ACTIVE` (`partnership_not_active`); `SubscriptionGuard.require(company, NEW_ORDER)`. |
| ORD-002 | Ҳар сатр: product `is_active`, unit `is_active`, нарх `PriceService.resolve(terms.price_list_id, unit, now)` ≠ None → вагарна `product_not_orderable` (бо `details.product_unit_id`). |
| ORD-003 | Миқдор: `≥ min_order_qty`; агар `allow_fraction = false` → бутун; ҳадди аксар 3 рақами даҳӣ (`quantity_invalid`). |
| ORD-004 | ≤ 200 сатр дар як order; сатрҳои такрорӣ (ҳамон unit) якҷоя мешаванд. |
| ORD-005 | `requested_subtotal ≥ terms.minimum_order_amount` → вагарна `minimum_order_not_met` (огоҳии барвақтӣ; санҷиши асосӣ дар ORD-022). |
| ORD-006 | Stock ҳангоми сохтан **захира намешавад** ва сохтанро манъ намекунад; каталог танҳо нишондиҳандаи дастрасӣ медиҳад (ORD-040). |
| ORD-007 | `unit_price` дар вақти сохтан snapshot мешавад ва баъдан тағйир намеёбад (store ҳамон нархеро мепардозад, ки дид). |
| ORD-008 | Checkout аз cart: сохтани order ва холӣ кардани сатрҳои cart барои ҳамон partnership дар як transaction. Idempotency-Key ҳатмӣ. |
| ORD-009 | On-behalf: `source = COMPANY_ON_BEHALF`, `created_by` = user-и company; ҳамон қоидаҳо; store notification мегирад. |

#### 3.2. Confirmation (ORD-020 … ORD-026)

Ҳама дар **як transaction**, бо ин тартиб:

| ID | Қадам |
|---|---|
| ORD-020 | Lock order; status ∈ {NEW, VIEWED}; `version` мувофиқ; partnership ∈ {ACTIVE, SUSPENDED}; `SubscriptionGuard.require(ORDER_FULFILLMENT)`. |
| ORD-021 | `lines`: барои **ҳар** item қимат (`0 ≤ confirmed ≤ requested`, қоидаи fraction). Ҳама 0 → истифодаи reject лозим (`validation_error`). |
| ORD-022 | Terms-и ҷорӣ → `terms_id`, `terms_snapshot`. `subtotal = Σ q2(confirmed × unit_price)`. `delivery_fee` — DEC-27 (аз `subtotal`). `discount` — DEC-26 (танҳо OWNER/MANAGER; вагарна `permission_denied`). `total = subtotal − discount + delivery_fee`. Агар `subtotal < minimum_order_amount` → `minimum_order_not_met`, магар OWNER/MANAGER `override_minimum_reason` диҳад. |
| ORD-023 | Credit: `CreditPort.check(partnership_id, total)` (P09) — DEC-09. Ноком → `credit_limit_exceeded` бо details, магар OWNER/MANAGER `override_credit_reason` диҳад (audit `order.credit_override`). |
| ORD-024 | `StockService.reserve(company, [(product_id, q3(confirmed × coefficient))…], source=ORDER)`. Нокифоя → `insufficient_stock` (ҳама rollback). |
| ORD-025 | Status → CONFIRMED ё PARTIALLY_CONFIRMED; `confirmed_by/at`; history; audit; event. |
| ORD-026 | Фарқи `requested − confirmed` ҳеҷ гоҳ захира, ирсол ё ҳисоб карда намешавад (flow §146 v3). |

#### 3.3. Cancellation (DEC-12)

| ID | Қоида |
|---|---|
| ORD-030 | Store: танҳо дар NEW/VIEWED; S.OWNER — ҳар order; S.SELLER — танҳо order-ҳое, ки худаш сохтааст. |
| ORD-031 | Company: OWNER/MANAGER то READY_FOR_DELIVERY (T9) ва аз DELIVERY_FAILED (T14), бо `reason`. Аз IN_TRANSIT — манъ (`cancellation_not_allowed`). |
| ORD-032 | Cancel пас аз confirmation: `StockService.release` дар ҳамон transaction; агар delivery-и фаъол бошад — `DeliveryCancellationPort.cancel_for_order` (P08). Таъсири молиявӣ нест (charge ҳанӯз нест — DEC-06). |

#### 3.4. Дигар

| ID | Қоида |
|---|---|
| ORD-040 | Каталоги store: `availability_status` = `IN_STOCK` (available ≥ threshold ё threshold NULL ва available > 0), `LOW` (0 < available < threshold), `OUT_OF_STOCK` (available ≤ 0). Рақами дақиқи stock ба store нишон дода намешавад. |
| ORD-041 | Completion job (ҳар 30 дақ): DELIVERED ва `delivered_at + terms_snapshot.dispute_window_hours ≤ now` ва `OpenDisputePort.has_open(order_id) = false` → T16. Idempotent. |
| ORD-042 | IDOR: company order-ҳои `company_id = org`-ро мебинад; store — `store_id = org`. Дигар → 404. |
| ORD-043 | WAREHOUSE order-ҳоро аз CONFIRMED ба боло мебинад, **бе нархҳо** (schema-и алоҳида `OrderWarehouseView`). |
| ORD-044 | «Такрори order»: store сатрҳои order-и кӯҳнаро ба cart илова мекунад (нарх аз нав ҳисоб мешавад; сатрҳои фармоишнашаванда бо огоҳӣ партофта мешаванд). |

| Port | Default дар P07 | Implementation |
|---|---|---|
| `CreditPort.check(partnership_id, amount) -> CreditCheck{allowed, limit, outstanding, unapplied}` | `outstanding=0, unapplied=0` — танҳо бо `credit_limit` | P09 |
| `DeliveryCancellationPort.cancel_for_order(order_id)` | no-op | P08 |
| `OpenDisputePort.has_open(order_id)` | `False` | P10 |
| `OrderCancellationPort` (аз P06) | — | ҳоло воқеӣ: `cancel_open_for_partnership` |

### 7. Audit ва events

Audit: `order.created`, `order.transition` (from/to/reason), `order.credit_override`, `order.minimum_override`, `order.discount_applied`.
Events: мувофиқи §2 (`ORDER_CREATED`, `ORDER_CONFIRMED`, `ORDER_REJECTED`, `ORDER_CANCELLED`, `ORDER_ASSEMBLING`, `ORDER_READY`, `ORDER_DISPATCHED`, `ORDER_DELIVERED`, `ORDER_DELIVERY_FAILED`, `ORDER_DISPUTED`, `ORDER_COMPLETED`). Payload: `order_id, order_number, company_id, store_id, partnership_id, status, total`.

## [P08_delivery] P08 — DELIVERY

**Вобастагӣ:** P00–P07
**Мақсад:** delivery барои ҳар order, delivery run-ҳои courier, тасдиқ бо коди 6-рақама, courier PWA бо offline queue ва sync-и idempotent.

---

### 1. Data Model

#### 1.1. `delivery_runs`

| Field | Type | Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| id | UUID | PK |
| company_id | UUID | FK |
| courier_id | UUID | FK users (membership COURIER-и ҳамон company, ё OWNER/MANAGER) |
| run_date | DATE | NOT NULL (санаи маҳаллӣ) |
| status | VARCHAR(16) | CHECK IN (`DRAFT`,`STARTED`,`FINISHED`,`CANCELLED`) |
| started_at / finished_at | timestamptz | NULL |
| created_by | UUID | FK |
| created_at / updated_at / version | | |

Index (`company_id`, `run_date`), (`courier_id`, `run_date`).

#### 1.2. `deliveries`

| Field | Type | Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| id | UUID | PK |
| company_id / store_id | UUID | FK (denormalized) |
| order_id | UUID | FK orders |
| attempt_no | SMALLINT | CHECK ≥ 1; UNIQUE (`order_id`, `attempt_no`) |
| status | VARCHAR(16) | CHECK IN (`PLANNED`,`ASSIGNED`,`IN_TRANSIT`,`ARRIVED`,`DELIVERED`,`FAILED`,`CANCELLED`) |
| run_id | UUID | NULL FK delivery_runs |
| stop_sequence | SMALLINT | NULL; UNIQUE (`run_id`, `stop_sequence`) |
| courier_id | UUID | NULL FK users |
| address / latitude / longitude | | snapshot аз order |
| code_hash | CHAR(64) | NULL — HMAC-SHA256(`DELIVERY_CODE_HMAC_SECRET`, `delivery_id:code`) |
| code_encrypted | BYTEA | NULL — AES-256-GCM (калиди алоҳида) барои нишон додан ба store (DEL-012) |
| code_attempts | SMALLINT | DEFAULT 0 |
| code_locked | BOOLEAN | DEFAULT false |
| confirmation_method | VARCHAR(16) | NULL; CHECK IN (`CODE`,`MANUAL_OVERRIDE`) |
| manual_reason | TEXT | NULL |
| failure_reason_code | VARCHAR(24) | NULL; CHECK IN (`STORE_CLOSED`,`REFUSED`,`ADDRESS_NOT_FOUND`,`NO_CONTACT`,`VEHICLE_ISSUE`,`OTHER`) |
| failure_note | TEXT | NULL |
| assigned_at / dispatched_at / arrived_at / delivered_at / failed_at / cancelled_at | timestamptz | NULL |
| created_at / updated_at / version | | |

Partial UNIQUE (`order_id`) WHERE `status IN ('PLANNED','ASSIGNED','IN_TRANSIT','ARRIVED')` — як delivery-и фаъол барои order.

#### 1.3. `delivery_status_history` (append-only)

`id, delivery_id, from_status, to_status, actor_id, actor_type, reason, source (ONLINE|OFFLINE_SYNC), metadata, created_at`.

#### 1.4. `courier_sync_operations`

| Field | Type | Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| id | UUID | PK = `operation_id` аз client |
| courier_id | UUID | FK |
| operation_type | VARCHAR(24) | CHECK IN (`DELIVERY_ARRIVE`,`DELIVERY_CONFIRM`,`DELIVERY_FAIL`,`PAYMENT_RECORD`) |
| entity_id | UUID | NOT NULL |
| payload | JSONB | NOT NULL (бе коди плейн — DEL-021) |
| client_created_at | timestamptz | NOT NULL |
| received_at | timestamptz | NOT NULL |
| result_status | VARCHAR(16) | CHECK IN (`APPLIED`,`DUPLICATE`,`CONFLICT`,`REJECTED`) |
| result | JSONB | NOT NULL |

Нигоҳдорӣ 30 рӯз (Celery cleanup).

---

### 2. State machines

#### 2.1. Delivery

| From | To | Actor | Шартҳо | Side effects |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| — | PLANNED | SYSTEM (handler `ORDER_READY`) | Delivery-и фаъол нест | `attempt_no = max + 1`; address snapshot |
| PLANNED | ASSIGNED | C.OWNER/MANAGER | Courier membership ACTIVE (COURIER/OWNER/MANAGER) | `courier_id`, `run_id?`, `stop_sequence?` |
| ASSIGNED | PLANNED | C.OWNER/MANAGER | Run ҳанӯз `DRAFT` ё нест | `courier_id = NULL`, аз run хориҷ |
| ASSIGNED | IN_TRANSIT | Courier-и таъиншуда ё OWNER/MANAGER (ё автоматӣ ҳангоми run start) | `SubscriptionGuard.require(ORDER_FULFILLMENT)` | Код генератсия (DEL-010); `OrderService.dispatch` (T10); event `DELIVERY_DISPATCHED` |
| IN_TRANSIT | ARRIVED | Courier / OWNER/MANAGER | — | `arrived_at` |
| IN_TRANSIT, ARRIVED | DELIVERED | Courier бо код / OWNER/MANAGER бо код ё manual override | DEL-011/013; `ORDER_CLOSE` | `OrderService.deliver` (T11) → P09 charge; event `DELIVERY_COMPLETED` |
| IN_TRANSIT, ARRIVED | FAILED | Courier / OWNER/MANAGER | `failure_reason_code` (+ `note` агар OTHER) | `OrderService.fail_delivery` (T12); event `DELIVERY_FAILED` |
| PLANNED, ASSIGNED | CANCELLED | SYSTEM (order cancel) ё OWNER/MANAGER | — | Агар дар run — хориҷ |

DELIVERED, FAILED, CANCELLED — ниҳоӣ. Кӯшиши нав (T13 дар P07) delivery-и **нав** месозад.

#### 2.2. Delivery run

| From | To | Actor | Шартҳо | Side effects |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| — | DRAFT | OWNER/MANAGER | Deliveries ∈ PLANNED ё ASSIGNED ба ҳамон courier | Deliveries → ASSIGNED бо тартиби `stop_sequence` |
| DRAFT | STARTED | Courier-и run ё OWNER/MANAGER | ≥ 1 delivery | Ҳамаи ASSIGNED → IN_TRANSIT (ҳар кадом дар як transaction-и умумӣ) |
| STARTED | FINISHED | Courier ё OWNER/MANAGER | Ҳамаи deliveries ниҳоӣ | — |
| DRAFT | CANCELLED | OWNER/MANAGER | — | Deliveries → PLANNED |

Дар DRAFT тартиб ва ҳайати run таҳрир мешавад; пас аз STARTED ҳайат ва тартиб тағйир намеёбанд (`run_not_editable`).

---

### 3. Business rules

| ID | Қоида |
|---|---|
| DEL-001 | Handler-и синхронии `ORDER_READY` delivery-и PLANNED месозад (ҳам T8, ҳам T13). |
| DEL-002 | `DeliveryCancellationPort` (P07) — ҳоло воқеӣ: delivery-и фаъолро CANCELLED мекунад; агар IN_TRANSIT/ARRIVED бошад → хато (нигаред ORD-031). |
| DEL-003 | Courier танҳо delivery-ҳои ба худ таъиншударо мебинад ва амал мекунад; дигар → 404. |
| DEL-004 | OWNER/MANAGER метавонанд ҳар амали courier-ро иҷро кунанд (барои company-ҳои бе courier). |
| DEL-010 | Код: 6 рақам аз `secrets.randbelow`; ҳангоми `→ IN_TRANSIT` генератсия; `code_hash` + `code_encrypted`; `code_attempts = 0`. |
| DEL-011 | Санҷиш: `hmac.compare_digest`. Нодуруст → `code_attempts++`, `422 delivery_code_invalid` бо `attempts_left`; 5-ум нодуруст → `code_locked = true`, `409 delivery_code_locked`, event `DELIVERY_CODE_LOCKED` (→ MANAGER). Rate limit `delivery_confirm`. |
| DEL-012 | Код ба store тавассути app (`GET /orders/{id}/delivery` — танҳо барои S.OWNER/SELLER, танҳо дар IN_TRANSIT/ARRIVED) ва notification (P11) нишон дода мешавад. Код ҳеҷ гоҳ дар log, audit, outbox payload, response-и company/courier нест. |
| DEL-013 | Manual override: OWNER/MANAGER, `reason ≥ 10` аломат, `confirmation_method = MANUAL_OVERRIDE`, audit `delivery.manual_confirm`, event `DELIVERY_MANUAL_CONFIRMED` (→ store OWNER барои огоҳӣ). |
| DEL-014 | Regenerate: OWNER/MANAGER; танҳо дар IN_TRANSIT/ARRIVED; коди нав, attempts 0, unlock; audit; notification-и нав ба store. |
| DEL-015 | Delivery → DELIVERED ва Order → DELIVERED ва Stock SHIP ва Charge (P09) — **дар як transaction**. |
| DEL-016 | Navigation: линки берунӣ `https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&destination={lat},{lng}` (ё бо address агар координата нест). Map provider-и худӣ нест. |

#### 3.1. Offline sync (DEL-020 … DEL-026)

| ID | Қоида |
|---|---|
| DEL-020 | Offline танҳо дар courier PWA (DEC-24). Client ҳар амалро ба IndexedDB queue менависад: `{operation_id (UUIDv7), operation_type, entity_id, payload, expected_status, client_created_at, retry_count, status}`. |
| DEL-021 | `DELIVERY_CONFIRM` offline: код дар queue **рамзгузоришуда** бо калиди session (WebCrypto) нигоҳ дошта мешавад; дар payload-и сервер сабт намешавад; server онро ҳангоми sync месанҷад. |
| DEL-022 | `POST /courier/sync`: ≤ 100 operation; сервер бо тартиби `client_created_at` коркард мекунад, **ҳар operation дар transaction-и алоҳида**. |
| DEL-023 | Idempotency: `operation_id`-и аллакай мавҷуд → ҳамон `result` бо `result_status = DUPLICATE`. |
| DEL-024 | Server authority: агар ҳолати ҷорӣ бо `expected_status` мувофиқ набошад ва амал аз ҳолати ҷорӣ иҷозат набошад → `CONFLICT` бо `server_state` (`sync_conflict`). Client server state-ро қабул мекунад ва ба courier нишон медиҳад; overwrite манъ. |
| DEL-025 | Агар амал аз ҳолати ҷорӣ иҷозат бошад (масалан ARRIVE аллакай иҷро шуда, CONFIRM омад) → `APPLIED`. |
| DEL-026 | Натиҷаҳо: `APPLIED`, `DUPLICATE`, `CONFLICT`, `REJECTED` (validation/permission/код нодуруст). Client operation-ҳои `REJECTED`/`CONFLICT`-ро аз queue мебарорад ва дар экрани «Мушкилот» нишон медиҳад. |

`PAYMENT_RECORD` дар P09 фаъол мешавад; то он вақт → `REJECTED` бо `not_supported`.

| `delivery.view_store` (+ код) | — | — | — | — | — | ✓ | ✓ |

### 5. API

#### 5.1. Company

| Method | Path | Idempotent | Тавсиф |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/v1/deliveries` | — | filter `status`, `courier_id`, `run_id`, `date`, `unassigned=true` |
| GET | `/api/v1/deliveries/{id}` | — | + history |
| POST | `/api/v1/deliveries/{id}/assign` | — | `{courier_id, version}` |
| POST | `/api/v1/deliveries/{id}/unassign` | — | |
| POST | `/api/v1/deliveries/{id}/cancel` | — | `{reason}` (танҳо PLANNED/ASSIGNED; order-ро cancel намекунад) |
| POST | `/api/v1/deliveries/{id}/regenerate-code` | ✓ | |
| POST | `/api/v1/deliveries/{id}/manual-confirm` | ✓ | `{reason}` |
| GET/POST | `/api/v1/delivery-runs` | ✓ (POST) | POST: `{courier_id, run_date, delivery_ids: [ordered]}` |
| GET | `/api/v1/delivery-runs/{id}` | — | Stops бо тартиб |
| PATCH | `/api/v1/delivery-runs/{id}` | — | DRAFT: `{delivery_ids, version}` |
| POST | `/api/v1/delivery-runs/{id}/start` / `finish` / `cancel` | ✓ | |

#### 5.2. Courier

| Method | Path | Idempotent | Тавсиф |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/v1/courier/today` | — | Run(ҳо)-и имрӯза бо stops: address, coords, store name/phone, order_number, items (бе нарх), `order_total`, status |
| GET | `/api/v1/courier/deliveries/{id}` | — | |
| POST | `/api/v1/courier/deliveries/{id}/dispatch` | ✓ | Агар бе run |
| POST | `/api/v1/courier/deliveries/{id}/arrive` | ✓ | |
| POST | `/api/v1/courier/deliveries/{id}/confirm` | ✓ | `{code}` |
| POST | `/api/v1/courier/deliveries/{id}/fail` | ✓ | `{reason_code, note?}` |
| POST | `/api/v1/courier/runs/{id}/start` / `finish` | ✓ | |
| POST | `/api/v1/courier/sync` | — (operation_id) | `{operations:[…]}` → `{results:[{operation_id, result_status, error?, server_state?}]}` |

#### 5.3. Store

| Method | Path | Тавсиф |
|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/v1/orders/{id}/delivery` | Ҳолат, courier name/phone (дар IN_TRANSIT/ARRIVED), `code` (DEL-012) |

### 6. Audit ва events

Audit: `delivery.assigned`, `.unassigned`, `.dispatched`, `.arrived`, `.delivered` (method), `.failed`, `.cancelled`, `.code_regenerated`, `.code_locked`, `.manual_confirm`, `run.*`.
Events: `DELIVERY_ASSIGNED` (→ courier), `DELIVERY_DISPATCHED` (→ store бо код — consumer код-ро аз `code_encrypted` мегирад), `DELIVERY_COMPLETED`, `DELIVERY_FAILED`, `DELIVERY_CODE_LOCKED`, `DELIVERY_MANUAL_CONFIRMED`.

---

### 7. Frontend

#### Company

| Экран | Route | Тавсиф |
|---|---|---|
| Delivery board | `/company/delivery` | Чап: PLANNED-и таъиннашуда (store, address, total); рост: run-ҳо барои сана (интихоби сана); сохтани run: интихоби courier + deliveries, тартиб бо drag; start |
| Run detail | `/company/delivery/runs/:id` | Stops бо ҳолат ва вақт; амалҳо |
| Delivery detail | `/company/delivery/:id` | Timeline, courier, амалҳо: assign, cancel, regenerate code, manual confirm (dialog бо reason ва огоҳии сурх) |

#### Courier (PWA, mobile-first)

| Экран | Route | Тавсиф |
|---|---|---|
| Имрӯз | `/courier` | Рӯйхати stops бо тартиб; нишондиҳандаи online/offline ва шумораи амалҳои дар навбат; «Оғози run» |
| Stop | `/courier/stops/:deliveryId` | Store, address, «Харита» (линки берунӣ), «Занг» (`tel:`), items, ҷамъ; «Расидам»; «Супоридам» → keypad-и 6-рақама; «Нашуд» → reason |
| Мушкилот | `/courier/issues` | Operation-ҳои CONFLICT/REJECTED бо ҳолати server |

Service worker: cache-и `/courier/today` ва assets; sync ҳангоми `online` event ва ҳар 30 сония; retry бо backoff; навбат ҳангоми logout тоза намешавад, то sync нашавад (огоҳӣ).

#### Store

Order detail: блоки «Расонидан» — ҳолат, courier, **код бо рақамҳои калон** ва матни «Ин кодро танҳо ҳангоми гирифтани мол ба курер гӯед».
