## [P09_finance] P09 — FINANCE (Charges, Payments, Allocation, Ledger, Credit)

**Вобастагӣ:** P00–P08
**Мақсад:** модели молиявии пурра ва immutable: charge ҳангоми delivery, payment бо тасдиқ, FIFO allocation, ledger-и append-only, adjustment бо approval, credit control, overdue, reconciliation.

> Ин қисм приоритети 2-юм аст (Financial Integrity). Ҳар қоида бояд test дошта бошад.

---

### 1. Модели консептуалӣ

```text
Қарз (debit items)            Кредит (credit items)
┌──────────────┐              ┌──────────────┐
│ charges      │              │ credits      │
│  ORDER       │   allocations│  PAYMENT     │
│  ADJUSTMENT  │◄────────────►│  CREDIT_NOTE │
│  REFUND      │  (FIFO)      │  ADJUSTMENT  │
└──────┬───────┘              └──────┬───────┘
       │ DEBIT                       │ CREDIT
       └────────► ledger_entries ◄───┘
                        │
               partnership_balances (projection)
```

**Қоидаи аломат (FIN-001):** `amount` ҳамеша мусбат; `direction ∈ {DEBIT, CREDIT}`. DEBIT қарзи store-ро зиёд мекунад, CREDIT кам. `balance = Σ DEBIT − Σ CREDIT`. `balance > 0` — store қарздор; `balance < 0` — store кредит дорад (пешпардохт/барзиёдӣ).

**Invariant (FIN-002):** `balance = outstanding − unapplied`, ки `outstanding = Σ (charge.amount − charge.allocated_amount)`, `unapplied = Σ (credit.amount − credit.allocated_amount)`.

---

### 2. Data Model

#### 2.1. `charges`

| Field | Type | Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| id | UUID | PK |
| partnership_id / company_id / store_id | UUID | FK |
| kind | VARCHAR(16) | CHECK IN (`ORDER`,`ADJUSTMENT`,`REFUND`) |
| source_id | UUID | NOT NULL (order_id / adjustment_id) |
| amount | NUMERIC(12,2) | CHECK > 0 |
| currency | CHAR(3) | |
| due_date | DATE | NOT NULL |
| allocated_amount | NUMERIC(12,2) | DEFAULT 0; CHECK 0 ≤ allocated ≤ amount |
| status | VARCHAR(16) | CHECK IN (`OPEN`,`PARTIALLY_PAID`,`PAID`) — мувофиқи allocated |
| created_at | timestamptz | |
| paid_at | timestamptz | NULL |

UNIQUE (`kind`, `source_id`) — як charge барои як order (FIN-010). Trigger: UPDATE танҳо `allocated_amount`, `status`, `paid_at`; DELETE манъ.
Index: (`partnership_id`, `status`, `due_date`, `created_at`).

#### 2.2. `credits`

| Field | Type | Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| id | UUID | PK |
| partnership_id / company_id / store_id | UUID | FK |
| kind | VARCHAR(16) | CHECK IN (`PAYMENT`,`CREDIT_NOTE`,`ADJUSTMENT`) |
| source_id | UUID | NOT NULL |
| amount | NUMERIC(12,2) | CHECK > 0 |
| allocated_amount | NUMERIC(12,2) | CHECK 0 ≤ allocated ≤ amount |
| created_at | timestamptz | |

UNIQUE (`kind`, `source_id`). Trigger монанд.

#### 2.3. `allocations` (append-only)

| Field | Type | Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| id | UUID | PK |
| partnership_id | UUID | FK |
| charge_id | UUID | FK charges |
| credit_id | UUID | FK credits |
| amount | NUMERIC(12,2) | CHECK > 0 |
| created_at | timestamptz | |

Trigger `forbid_mutation`. Charge ва credit бояд як `partnership_id` дошта бошанд (trigger).

#### 2.4. `ledger_entries` (append-only)

| Field | Type | Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| id | UUID | PK |
| partnership_id / company_id / store_id | UUID | FK |
| entry_type | VARCHAR(24) | CHECK IN (`CHARGE`,`PAYMENT`,`CREDIT_NOTE`,`ADJUSTMENT_DEBIT`,`ADJUSTMENT_CREDIT`,`REFUND`) |
| direction | VARCHAR(6) | CHECK IN (`DEBIT`,`CREDIT`); мувофиқат бо entry_type (CHECK) |
| amount | NUMERIC(12,2) | CHECK > 0 |
| currency | CHAR(3) | |
| source_type | VARCHAR(16) | `ORDER`,`PAYMENT`,`CREDIT_NOTE`,`ADJUSTMENT` |
| source_id | UUID | NOT NULL |
| balance_after | NUMERIC(12,2) | NOT NULL |
| description | VARCHAR(255) | масалан «Order ORD-2026-000123» |
| created_by | UUID | NULL |
| created_at | timestamptz | |

UNIQUE (`entry_type`, `source_id`) — ҳимоя аз такрор (FIN-011). Index (`partnership_id`, `created_at`, `id`). Trigger `forbid_mutation`.

Мувофиқати direction: DEBIT ← `CHARGE, ADJUSTMENT_DEBIT, REFUND`; CREDIT ← `PAYMENT, CREDIT_NOTE, ADJUSTMENT_CREDIT`.

#### 2.5. `partnership_balances` (projection)

`partnership_id PK, balance NUMERIC(12,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, last_entry_id UUID NULL, updated_at, version`. Сатр ҳангоми `PARTNERSHIP_ACTIVATED` (handler) ё аввалин entry сохта мешавад.

#### 2.6. `payments`

| Field | Type | Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| id | UUID | PK |
| partnership_id / company_id / store_id | UUID | FK |
| amount | NUMERIC(12,2) | CHECK > 0 |
| currency | CHAR(3) | |
| method | VARCHAR(16) | CHECK IN (`CASH`,`BANK_TRANSFER`) |
| reference | VARCHAR(100) | NULL (ҳатмӣ барои BANK_TRANSFER) |
| status | VARCHAR(16) | CHECK IN (`PENDING`,`CONFIRMED`,`REJECTED`,`CANCELLED`) |
| recorded_by | UUID | FK users |
| recorded_side | VARCHAR(8) | `COMPANY`/`STORE` |
| delivery_id | UUID | NULL FK deliveries (нақди courier) |
| note | TEXT | NULL |
| confirmed_by / confirmed_at | | NULL |
| rejected_reason | TEXT | NULL |
| cancelled_at | timestamptz | NULL |
| created_at / updated_at / version | | |

Index (`company_id`, `status`, `created_at DESC`), (`partnership_id`, `created_at DESC`).

#### 2.7. `payment_status_history` (append-only)

`id, payment_id, from_status, to_status, actor_id, reason, created_at`.

#### 2.8. `adjustments`

| Field | Type | Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| id | UUID | PK |
| partnership_id | UUID | FK |
| type | VARCHAR(8) | CHECK IN (`DEBIT`,`CREDIT`,`REFUND`) |
| amount | NUMERIC(12,2) | CHECK > 0 |
| reason | TEXT | NOT NULL, ≥ 10 аломат |
| source | VARCHAR(16) | CHECK IN (`MANUAL`,`DISPUTE`) |
| source_id | UUID | NULL (dispute_id) |
| status | VARCHAR(20) | CHECK IN (`PENDING_APPROVAL`,`APPROVED`,`REJECTED`) |
| created_by | UUID | FK |
| approved_by / approved_at | | NULL |
| self_approved | BOOLEAN | DEFAULT false |
| rejected_reason | TEXT | NULL |
| created_at / updated_at / version | | |

#### 2.9. `credit_notes`

`id, partnership_id, amount CHECK > 0, source_type ('RETURN'), source_id UNIQUE, created_by, created_at` — дар P10 сохта мешаванд тавассути `FinanceService.create_credit_note`.

#### 2.10. Тағйир дар `companies`

Migration column илова мекунад: `debt_reminders_enabled BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true`.

---

### 3. FinanceService (ягона дарвозаи молиявӣ)

```python
class FinanceService:
    # debit
    async def create_order_charge(system, order) -> Charge | None          # handler ORDER_DELIVERED
    # payments
    async def record_payment(ctx, partnership_id, amount, method, reference, note, delivery_id, confirm: bool) -> Payment
    async def confirm_payment(ctx, payment_id, version) -> Payment
    async def reject_payment(ctx, payment_id, reason, version) -> Payment
    async def cancel_payment(ctx, payment_id, version) -> Payment
    # adjustments
    async def create_adjustment(ctx, partnership_id, type, amount, reason, source, source_id) -> Adjustment
    async def approve_adjustment(ctx, id, version) / reject_adjustment(ctx, id, reason, version)
    # credit notes (P10)
    async def create_credit_note(system, partnership_id, amount, source_id) -> CreditNote
    # queries
    async def balance(partnership_id) -> BalanceSummary
    async def credit_check(partnership_id, amount) -> CreditCheck          # CreditPort
    async def allocation_preview(partnership_id, amount) -> list[AllocationLine]
    # internal
    async def _post_ledger(...)  # lock balance → insert entry → update balance
    async def _allocate_fifo(partnership_id)
```

Ҳар метод: як transaction; аввал `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` бар `partnership_balances` (сериализатсия барои ҳар partnership — FIN-003); сипас амалиёт.

---

### 4. Business rules

#### 4.1. Ledger ва баланс

| ID | Қоида |
|---|---|
| FIN-001 | Қоидаи аломат — §1. |
| FIN-002 | Invariant — §1; reconciliation (FIN-060). |
| FIN-003 | Ҳар `_post_ledger`: lock `partnership_balances` → `balance_after = balance ± amount` → INSERT entry → UPDATE balance. Ду амали параллелӣ барои як partnership пайдарпай иҷро мешаванд. |
| FIN-004 | Ledger, allocations, charges, credits, credit_notes — delete/update-и деструктивӣ манъ (trigger). Ислоҳ танҳо тавассути adjustment (P03 принсип). |
| FIN-005 | Ҳисобҳои молиявӣ (dashboard, reports, credit check) **танҳо** аз `FinanceService` (як манбаъ; §91 v3). |

#### 4.2. Charges

| ID | Қоида |
|---|---|
| FIN-010 | Handler-и синхронии `ORDER_DELIVERED`: `charge(kind=ORDER, amount=order.total, due_date = local_date(delivered_at) + terms_snapshot.credit_days)` + ledger `CHARGE` + FIFO. Агар `order.total = 0` — charge сохта намешавад (log). |
| FIN-011 | Такрори event (ё retry) charge-и дуюм намесозад: UNIQUE (`kind`, `source_id`) ва UNIQUE-и ledger. |
| FIN-012 | Timezone-и ҳисоби `due_date`: `Asia/Dushanbe`. |

#### 4.3. Payments

| # | From | To | Actor | Шартҳо | Side effects |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| P1 | — | PENDING | C.OWNER/MANAGER/OPERATOR/COURIER ё S.OWNER | FIN-020 | event `PAYMENT_RECORDED` |
| P2 | PENDING | CONFIRMED | C.OWNER/MANAGER | — | credit(PAYMENT) + ledger PAYMENT + FIFO; event `PAYMENT_CONFIRMED` |
| P3 | PENDING | REJECTED | C.OWNER/MANAGER | `reason` | event `PAYMENT_REJECTED` |
| P4 | PENDING | CANCELLED | Худи сабткунанда | — | — |

Агар C.OWNER/MANAGER бо `confirm=true` сабт кунад → P1 ва P2 дар як transaction (ду сатри history).

| ID | Қоида |
|---|---|
| FIN-020 | `amount > 0`, ≤ 2 рақами даҳӣ, ≤ 10 000 000.00; `method ∈ terms_current.payment_methods`; BANK_TRANSFER → `reference` ҳатмӣ. Partnership ∈ {ACTIVE, SUSPENDED, TERMINATED}. |
| FIN-021 | `Idempotency-Key` ҳатмӣ барои record ва confirm (flow §147 v3: як payment, як ledger entry, як маҷмӯи allocation). |
| FIN-022 | Payment-и CONFIRMED тағйирнопазир ва бекорнашаванда (DEC-11). |
| FIN-023 | Courier: танҳо `CASH`, `delivery_id` ҳатмӣ ва delivery ба ӯ таъин шуда бошад; offline op `PAYMENT_RECORD` (P08) ҳоло фаъол. |
| FIN-024 | `SubscriptionGuard` — `PAYMENT` ҳамеша иҷозат. |

#### 4.4. FIFO allocation

| ID | Қоида |
|---|---|
| FIN-030 | `_allocate_fifo(partnership_id)` пас аз ҳар charge ё credit-и нав дар ҳамон transaction иҷро мешавад. |
| FIN-031 | Тартиб: charges-и кушода аз рӯи (`due_date`, `created_at`, `id`) ↑; credits-и дорои бақия аз рӯи (`created_at`, `id`) ↑. Ҳарду бо `FOR UPDATE`. Алгоритми greedy: `x = min(charge.remaining, credit.remaining)` → allocation → навсозии `allocated_amount` ва `status`. |
| FIN-032 | Натиҷа детерминистӣ аст (ҳамон вуруд → ҳамон allocations). |
| FIN-033 | Overpayment (DEC-10): бақияи credit ҳамчун unapplied мемонад ва ба charge-ҳои оянда автоматӣ тақсим мешавад. |
| FIN-034 | Мисол (тест-и ҳатмӣ): charges 1000, 700, 500; payment 1200 → #1 PAID, #2 PARTIALLY_PAID (200), #3 OPEN. |

#### 4.5. Adjustments

| ID | Қоида |
|---|---|
| FIN-040 | Сохтан: C.OWNER/MANAGER; `reason` ≥ 10. OWNER сохт → фавран APPROVED (`self_approved = true`). MANAGER сохт → PENDING_APPROVAL; танҳо OWNER тасдиқ/рад мекунад. |
| FIN-041 | APPROVED `DEBIT` → charge(kind=ADJUSTMENT, due_date = санаи имрӯза) + ledger `ADJUSTMENT_DEBIT` + FIFO. |
| FIN-042 | APPROVED `CREDIT` → credit(kind=ADJUSTMENT) + ledger `ADJUSTMENT_CREDIT` + FIFO. |
| FIN-043 | APPROVED `REFUND` (company пулро ба store бармегардонад): `amount ≤ unapplied` → вагарна `refund_exceeds_credit`; charge(kind=REFUND) + ledger `REFUND` + FIFO (дарҳол бо unapplied баста мешавад). |
| FIN-044 | Adjustment-и `source = DISPUTE` аз P10 бо ҳамон қоидаҳо (approval-и OWNER). |

#### 4.6. Credit control ва overdue

| ID | Қоида |
|---|---|
| FIN-050 | `credit_check(partnership, amount)`: `allowed = balance + amount ≤ terms_current.credit_limit` (balance аллакай unapplied-ро ҳисоб мекунад) — DEC-09. Бармегардонад `{allowed, limit, balance, outstanding, unapplied, available = limit − balance}`. `CreditPort` (P07) ҳоло воқеӣ. |
| FIN-051 | Charge overdue аст, агар `due_date < today(Asia/Dushanbe)` ва `status ≠ PAID`. |
| FIN-052 | Aging: `current`, `1–30`, `31–60`, `61–90`, `90+` рӯз аз `due_date`. |
| FIN-053 | Job-и ҳаррӯза 09:00 (Asia/Dushanbe): event `DEBT_DUE_SOON` (як рӯз пеш) ва `DEBT_OVERDUE` (рӯзи аввали overdue, баъд ҳар 7 рӯз) — агар `companies.debt_reminders_enabled`; idempotent (ҷадвали `debt_reminders(charge_id, kind, anchor_date)` UNIQUE). |

#### 4.7. Reconciliation

| ID | Қоида |
|---|---|
| FIN-060 | Job-и ҳаррӯза 03:30: барои ҳар partnership санҷиш: (a) `partnership_balances.balance = Σ ledger`; (b) `balance = outstanding − unapplied`; (c) ҳар `charge.allocated_amount = Σ allocations`; (d) ҳар `credit.allocated_amount = Σ allocations`; (e) ҳар order-и DELIVERED/DISPUTED/COMPLETED бо `total > 0` charge дорад; (f) ҳар payment-и CONFIRMED credit ва ledger entry дорад. Ҳар mismatch → сатр дар `reconciliation_issues` + event `FINANCE_RECONCILIATION_MISMATCH` + Sentry. Ислоҳи автоматӣ **нест**. |

`reconciliation_issues`: `id, partnership_id, check_code, expected, actual, detected_at, resolved_at, resolved_by, note`.

---

### 5. Permission matrix (P09)

| Permission | C.OWNER | C.MANAGER | C.OPERATOR | C.WAREHOUSE | C.COURIER | S.OWNER | S.SELLER |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `finance.view` | ✓ | ✓ | ✓ | — | — | ✓ | — |
| `payments.record` | ✓ | ✓ | ✓ | — | ✓ (FIN-023) | ✓ | — |
| `payments.confirm` / `payments.reject` | ✓ | ✓ | — | — | — | — | — |
| `adjustments.create` | ✓ | ✓ | — | — | — | — | — |
| `adjustments.approve` | ✓ | — | — | — | — | — | — |

Store танҳо маълумоти partnership-ҳои худро мебинад; adjustments-и `PENDING_APPROVAL` ба store нишон дода намешаванд.

---

### 6. API

| Method | Path | Permission | Idempotent | Тавсиф |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/v1/finance/summary` | view | — | Company: ҷамъи дебиторӣ, overdue, aging; Store: ҷамъи қарз ба ҳар company |
| GET | `/api/v1/finance/partnerships` | view | — | Ҳар сатр: `balance, outstanding, overdue, unapplied, credit_limit, available`; filter `overdue=true`; ordering `balance`, `overdue` |
| GET | `/api/v1/finance/partnerships/{id}` | view | — | BalanceSummary + aging |
| GET | `/api/v1/finance/partnerships/{id}/charges` | view | — | filter `status`, `overdue` |
| GET | `/api/v1/finance/partnerships/{id}/statement` | view | — | `?date_from&date_to` → `opening_balance`, entries (бо `balance_after`), `closing_balance` |
| GET | `/api/v1/finance/partnerships/{id}/allocation-preview` | view | — | `?amount=` → рӯйхати charges ва маблағ (бе сабт) |
| GET | `/api/v1/finance/charges/{id}` | view | — | + allocations |
| GET | `/api/v1/payments` | view | — | filter `status`, `partnership_id`, `method`, `date_from/to` |
| POST | `/api/v1/payments` | record | ✓ | `{partnership_id, amount, method, reference?, note?, delivery_id?, confirm?}` |
| GET | `/api/v1/payments/{id}` | view | — | + history, allocations |
| POST | `/api/v1/payments/{id}/confirm` | confirm | ✓ | `{version}` |
| POST | `/api/v1/payments/{id}/reject` | reject | ✓ | `{reason, version}` |
| POST | `/api/v1/payments/{id}/cancel` | recorder | ✓ | `{version}` |
| GET | `/api/v1/adjustments` | view (company) | — | |
| POST | `/api/v1/adjustments` | create | ✓ | `{partnership_id, type, amount, reason}` |
| POST | `/api/v1/adjustments/{id}/approve` / `reject` | approve | ✓ | |
| GET | `/api/v1/admin/reconciliation-issues` | SUPERADMIN | — | |
| POST | `/api/v1/admin/reconciliation-issues/{id}/resolve` | SUPERADMIN | — | `{note}` |

---

### 7. Audit ва events

Audit: `charge.created`, `payment.recorded`, `payment.confirmed`, `payment.rejected`, `payment.cancelled`, `adjustment.created`, `adjustment.approved`, `adjustment.rejected`, `credit_note.created`, `reconciliation.resolved`.
Events: `CHARGE_CREATED`, `PAYMENT_RECORDED` (→ company), `PAYMENT_CONFIRMED` / `PAYMENT_REJECTED` (→ store), `ADJUSTMENT_PENDING_APPROVAL` (→ OWNER), `ADJUSTMENT_APPROVED` (→ store), `DEBT_DUE_SOON`, `DEBT_OVERDUE`, `FINANCE_RECONCILIATION_MISMATCH`.

---

### 8. Frontend

#### Company

| Экран | Route | Тавсиф |
|---|---|---|
| Дебиторӣ | `/company/finance` | Кортҳо: ҷамъи қарз, overdue, unapplied; диаграммаи aging (bar); ҷадвали store-ҳо (сурх агар overdue, норинҷӣ агар > 80% лимит) |
| Partner finance | `/company/finance/:partnershipId` | Кортҳо: баланс, лимит, дастрас; tabs: Charges (due, ҳолат, overdue badge), Payments, Выписка (statement бо баланси давон, давраи интихобӣ), Adjustments |
| Сабти пардохт | dialog | Маблағ, усул, reference, note; **preview-и FIFO** (кадом charges пӯшида мешаванд, бақияи unapplied) → «Сабт» ё «Сабт ва тасдиқ» (OWNER/MANAGER) |
| Пардохтҳо барои тасдиқ | `/company/finance/payments?status=PENDING` | Confirm (dialog бо такрори маблағ) / Reject (reason) |
| Adjustments | `/company/finance/adjustments` | Сохтан (type, маблағ, reason; preview-и таъсир ба баланс); навбати тасдиқ барои OWNER |

#### Store (S.OWNER)

| Экран | Route | Тавсиф |
|---|---|---|
| Қарзҳо | `/store/finance` | Ҳар таъминкунанда: баланс, overdue, лимит |
| Supplier finance | `/store/finance/:partnershipId` | Charges, payments (бо ҳолат), выписка |
| Хабари пардохт | dialog | BANK_TRANSFER/CASH (агар дар terms) + reference → PENDING |

#### Courier

Stop экран (P08): «Пули нақд гирифтам» → маблағ → PAYMENT_RECORD (offline-capable).

## [P10_returns_disputes] P10 — RETURNS & DISPUTES

**Вобастагӣ:** P00–P09
**Мақсад:** баргардонидани физикии мол бо credit note ва restock; баҳсҳо (disagreement) бо ҳалли назоратшаванда, ки ledger-ро мустақим тағйир намедиҳанд.

Return — ҳаракати физикӣ. Dispute — ихтилоф, на транзаксияи молиявӣ. Ҳалли dispute метавонад adjustment ё return эҷод кунад.

---

### 1. Data Model

#### 1.1. `returns`

| Field | Type | Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| id | UUID | PK |
| return_number | VARCHAR(20) | UNIQUE (`company_id`, …); `RET-2026-000045` |
| company_id / store_id / partnership_id | UUID | FK |
| order_id | UUID | FK |
| status | VARCHAR(16) | CHECK IN (`REQUESTED`,`APPROVED`,`REJECTED`,`CANCELLED`,`RECEIVED`,`COMPLETED`) |
| reason_code | VARCHAR(16) | CHECK IN (`DAMAGED`,`EXPIRED`,`WRONG_ITEM`,`NOT_ORDERED`,`QUALITY`,`OTHER`) |
| note | TEXT | NULL (ҳатмӣ агар OTHER) |
| source | VARCHAR(16) | `STORE_REQUEST` / `DISPUTE` |
| dispute_id | UUID | NULL |
| requested_by / approved_by / received_by / completed_by | UUID | NULL |
| rejection_reason / cancel_reason | TEXT | NULL |
| total_credit | NUMERIC(12,2) | NULL то COMPLETED |
| credit_note_id | UUID | NULL FK credit_notes |
| requested_at / approved_at / received_at / completed_at | timestamptz | |
| created_at / updated_at / version | | |

Partial UNIQUE (`order_id`) WHERE `status IN ('REQUESTED','APPROVED','RECEIVED')` — як return-и кушода барои order.

#### 1.2. `return_items`

| Field | Type | Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| id | UUID | PK |
| return_id | UUID | FK |
| order_item_id | UUID | FK; UNIQUE (`return_id`, `order_item_id`) |
| requested_quantity | NUMERIC(14,3) | CHECK > 0 (дар unit-и order item) |
| approved_quantity | NUMERIC(14,3) | NULL; ≤ requested |
| received_quantity | NUMERIC(14,3) | NULL; ≤ approved |
| accepted_quantity | NUMERIC(14,3) | NULL; ≤ received — барои credit |
| restock_quantity | NUMERIC(14,3) | NULL; ≤ accepted — ба анбор бармегардад |
| line_credit | NUMERIC(12,2) | NULL |

#### 1.3. `return_status_history` (append-only)

#### 1.4. `disputes`

| Field | Type | Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| id | UUID | PK |
| dispute_number | VARCHAR(20) | `DSP-2026-000012` |
| company_id / store_id / partnership_id | UUID | FK |
| target_type | VARCHAR(8) | CHECK IN (`ORDER`,`PAYMENT`) |
| order_id / payment_id | UUID | NULL; CHECK мувофиқи target_type |
| type | VARCHAR(16) | CHECK IN (`QUANTITY`,`PRICE`,`DAMAGED`,`DELIVERY`,`PAYMENT`,`OTHER`) |
| description | TEXT | NOT NULL, ≥ 10 |
| status | VARCHAR(16) | CHECK IN (`OPEN`,`UNDER_REVIEW`,`RESOLVED`,`REJECTED`,`WITHDRAWN`) |
| opened_by | UUID | FK |
| resolution_type | VARCHAR(24) | NULL; CHECK IN (`NO_ACTION`,`ADJUSTMENT_CREDIT`,`CONVERTED_TO_RETURN`) |
| resolution_note | TEXT | NULL |
| pending_adjustment_id / adjustment_id / return_id | UUID | NULL |
| resolved_by / resolved_at | | NULL |
| created_at / updated_at / version | | |

Partial UNIQUE (`order_id`) WHERE `status IN ('OPEN','UNDER_REVIEW')` — `dispute_already_open`.

#### 1.5. `dispute_messages` (append-only)

`id, dispute_id, author_id, author_side (COMPANY|STORE), body TEXT (≤ 2000), file_id NULL, created_at`.

---

### 2. Return state machine

| From | To | Actor | Шартҳо | Side effects |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| — | REQUESTED | S.OWNER | RET-001..004 | event `RETURN_REQUESTED` |
| — | APPROVED | SYSTEM (dispute) | DSP-022 | — |
| REQUESTED | APPROVED | C.OWNER/MANAGER | `approved_quantity` барои ҳар сатр (0 иҷозат, ҳама 0 → reject) | event `RETURN_APPROVED` |
| REQUESTED | REJECTED | C.OWNER/MANAGER | `reason` | event |
| REQUESTED | CANCELLED | S.OWNER | — | |
| APPROVED | CANCELLED | C.OWNER/MANAGER | `reason` | |
| APPROVED | RECEIVED | C.OWNER/MANAGER/WAREHOUSE | `received_quantity ≤ approved` | event `RETURN_RECEIVED` |
| RECEIVED | COMPLETED | C.OWNER/MANAGER | `accepted ≤ received`, `restock ≤ accepted` | RET-010..012; event `RETURN_COMPLETED` |

Логистикаи баргардонидан (кӣ молро меорад) дар MVP берун аз система аст; система танҳо қабулро сабт мекунад.

---

### 3. Return rules

| ID | Қоида |
|---|---|
| RET-001 | Order ∈ {DELIVERED, DISPUTED, COMPLETED}. |
| RET-002 | `now ≤ delivered_at + terms_snapshot.return_days` → вагарна `return_window_closed`. `return_days = 0` → return манъ. |
| RET-003 | Барои ҳар order item: `requested ≤ confirmed − Σ accepted (returns-и COMPLETED)` → вагарна `return_quantity_exceeds`. Қоидаи fraction-и unit. |
| RET-004 | Як return-и кушода барои order (constraint). |
| RET-010 | `line_credit = q2(accepted × unit_price × k)`, ки `k = (subtotal − discount) / subtotal` (discount-и order мутаносибан тақсим мешавад); delivery fee баргардонида намешавад. `total_credit = Σ line_credit`. |
| RET-011 | Агар `total_credit > 0` → `FinanceService.create_credit_note(partnership, total_credit, source_id=return.id)` → credit + ledger `CREDIT_NOTE` + FIFO — ҳамааш дар transaction-и COMPLETED. |
| RET-012 | `restock_quantity > 0` → `StockService.return_in(q3(restock × coefficient_snapshot), source=RETURN)`. Боқимонда (`accepted − restock`) — зарардида, ба анбор намеояд. |
| RET-013 | Return ҳолати order-ро тағйир намедиҳад. |
| RET-014 | `SubscriptionGuard` — `RETURNS_DISPUTES` ҳамеша иҷозат. |

---

### 4. Dispute state machine

| From | To | Actor | Шартҳо | Side effects |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| — | OPEN | S.OWNER | DSP-001..004 | ORDER: `OrderService.mark_disputed` (T15); event `DISPUTE_OPENED` |
| OPEN | UNDER_REVIEW | C.OWNER/MANAGER/OPERATOR | — | |
| OPEN, UNDER_REVIEW | RESOLVED | C.OWNER/MANAGER | Resolution: `NO_ACTION` ё `CONVERTED_TO_RETURN` | ORDER: `OrderService.complete` (T17) |
| UNDER_REVIEW | UNDER_REVIEW (+pending adjustment) | C.OWNER/MANAGER | `ADJUSTMENT_CREDIT` | DSP-021 |
| UNDER_REVIEW | RESOLVED | SYSTEM (handler `ADJUSTMENT_APPROVED`) | — | ORDER: T17 |
| OPEN, UNDER_REVIEW | REJECTED | C.OWNER/MANAGER | `resolution_note` | ORDER: T17 |
| OPEN, UNDER_REVIEW | WITHDRAWN | S.OWNER | — | ORDER: T17 |

---

### 5. Dispute rules

| ID | Қоида |
|---|---|
| DSP-001 | ORDER: order дар ҳолати DELIVERED ва `now ≤ delivered_at + dispute_window_hours` → вагарна `dispute_window_closed`. |
| DSP-002 | PAYMENT: payment-и ҳамон partnership, ҳар status, то 30 рӯз аз `created_at`. Ба payment таъсир намерасонад. |
| DSP-003 | Як dispute-и кушода барои order (`dispute_already_open`). |
| DSP-004 | `OpenDisputePort.has_open` (P07) ҳоло воқеӣ. |
| DSP-010 | Ҳарду тараф паём ва файл (VER-002 қоидаҳои файл) илова мекунанд; паёмҳо immutable. |
| DSP-020 | Dispute ledger-ро мустақим тағйир намедиҳад (§45 v3). |
| DSP-021 | `ADJUSTMENT_CREDIT`: `amount ≤ order.total` (ORDER) → `FinanceService.create_adjustment(type=CREDIT, source=DISPUTE)`. Агар OWNER → фавран APPROVED → dispute RESOLVED. Агар MANAGER → PENDING_APPROVAL, dispute дар UNDER_REVIEW бо `pending_adjustment_id`; handler-и `ADJUSTMENT_APPROVED` → RESOLVED; `ADJUSTMENT_REJECTED` → `pending_adjustment_id = NULL` + паёми системавӣ дар dispute. |
| DSP-022 | `CONVERTED_TO_RETURN`: return бо `source = DISPUTE`, ҳолати APPROVED, сатрҳо ва миқдорҳои додашуда (RET-003 санҷида мешавад, RET-002 **не** — dispute дар window кушода шуда буд). Dispute → RESOLVED. |
| DSP-023 | Эскалатсия ба платформа дар MVP нест; SUPERADMIN танҳо мебинад (P12). |
| DSP-024 | SLA: агар dispute 48 соат дар OPEN бошад → event `DISPUTE_SLA_WARNING` (→ C.OWNER/MANAGER), такрор ҳар 24 соат; idempotent. |

---

### 6. Permission matrix (P10)

| Permission | C.OWNER | C.MANAGER | C.OPERATOR | C.WAREHOUSE | C.COURIER | S.OWNER | S.SELLER |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `returns.view` | ✓ | ✓ | ✓ | ✓ | — | ✓ | ✓ |
| `returns.request` / `returns.cancel_own` | — | — | — | — | — | ✓ | — |
| `returns.approve` / `returns.complete` | ✓ | ✓ | — | — | — | — | — |
| `returns.receive` | ✓ | ✓ | — | ✓ | — | — | — |
| `disputes.view` | ✓ | ✓ | ✓ | — | — | ✓ | ✓ |
| `disputes.open` / `disputes.withdraw` | — | — | — | — | — | ✓ | — |
| `disputes.message` | ✓ | ✓ | ✓ | — | — | ✓ | — |
| `disputes.review` | ✓ | ✓ | ✓ | — | — | — | — |
| `disputes.resolve` | ✓ | ✓ | — | — | — | — | — |

---

### 7. API

| Method | Path | Idempotent | Тавсиф |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/v1/orders/{id}/returnable` | — | Барои ҳар item: `max_returnable`, `return_deadline` |
| GET/POST | `/api/v1/returns` | ✓ (POST) | POST: `{order_id, reason_code, note?, items:[{order_item_id, quantity}]}` |
| GET | `/api/v1/returns/{id}` | — | + history |
| POST | `/api/v1/returns/{id}/approve` | ✓ | `{items:[{id, approved_quantity}], version}` |
| POST | `/api/v1/returns/{id}/reject` / `cancel` | ✓ | `{reason, version}` |
| POST | `/api/v1/returns/{id}/receive` | ✓ | `{items:[{id, received_quantity}], version}` |
| GET | `/api/v1/returns/{id}/completion-preview` | — | `?items=` → `line_credit`, `total_credit` |
| POST | `/api/v1/returns/{id}/complete` | ✓ | `{items:[{id, accepted_quantity, restock_quantity}], version}` |
| GET/POST | `/api/v1/disputes` | ✓ (POST) | POST: `{target_type, order_id?, payment_id?, type, description, file_ids?}` |
| GET | `/api/v1/disputes/{id}` | — | + messages |
| POST | `/api/v1/disputes/{id}/messages` | ✓ | `{body, file_id?}` |
| POST | `/api/v1/disputes/{id}/start-review` | — | |
| POST | `/api/v1/disputes/{id}/resolve` | ✓ | `{resolution_type, resolution_note, amount?, return_items?, version}` |
| POST | `/api/v1/disputes/{id}/reject` | ✓ | `{resolution_note, version}` |
| POST | `/api/v1/disputes/{id}/withdraw` | ✓ | `{version}` |

---

### 8. Audit ва events

Audit: `return.*` (ҳар transition), `dispute.opened`, `.resolved`, `.rejected`, `.withdrawn`, `dispute.message_added`.
Events: `RETURN_REQUESTED`, `RETURN_APPROVED`, `RETURN_REJECTED`, `RETURN_RECEIVED`, `RETURN_COMPLETED`, `DISPUTE_OPENED`, `DISPUTE_MESSAGE`, `DISPUTE_RESOLVED`, `DISPUTE_REJECTED`, `DISPUTE_SLA_WARNING`.

---

### 9. Frontend

#### Store

| Экран | Тавсиф |
|---|---|
| Order detail: «Баргардонидан» | Намоён то `return_deadline`; форма: сабаб, сатрҳо бо `max_returnable`, note → preview → фиристодан |
| Order detail: «Шикоят» | Намоён дар DELIVERED то анҷоми window (таймери боқимонда); навъ, тавсиф, аксҳо |
| `/store/returns`, `/store/disputes` | Рӯйхат ва detail; dispute — chat бо паёмҳо ва файлҳо; withdraw |

#### Company

| Экран | Тавсиф |
|---|---|
| `/company/returns` | Tabs аз рӯи ҳолат. Detail: approve (таҳрири миқдорҳо), reject; receive (warehouse); complete: ҷадвал accepted/restock бо preview-и credit ва таъсир ба баланс → тасдиқ |
| `/company/disputes` | Навбат (SLA badge), detail бо chat, контексти order (items, delivery, код тасдиқ шуд ё manual), амалҳои resolve: NO_ACTION / ADJUSTMENT_CREDIT (маблағ + preview) / CONVERTED_TO_RETURN (интихоби сатрҳо) / REJECT |

---

## [P11_notifications_telegram] P11 — NOTIFICATIONS & TELEGRAM

**Вобастагӣ:** P00–P10
**Мақсад:** истеъмоли outbox events → notifications дар се канал (IN_APP, TELEGRAM, SMS); танзимоти user; Telegram bot (aiogram 3, webhook) бо пайвасти ҳисоб ва функсияҳои хондан (DEC-23); SMS provider-и воқеӣ.

---

### 1. Data Model

#### 1.1. `notifications`

| Field | Type | Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| id | UUID | PK |
| user_id | UUID | FK users |
| organization_id | UUID | NULL FK |
| event_id | UUID | FK outbox_events |
| event_type | VARCHAR(64) | NOT NULL |
| title_key / body_key | VARCHAR(128) | калидҳои тарҷума |
| params | JSONB | NOT NULL (бе маълумоти махфӣ) |
| link | VARCHAR(255) | NULL — route-и frontend (`/store/orders/<id>`) |
| read_at | timestamptz | NULL |
| created_at | timestamptz | |

UNIQUE (`event_id`, `user_id`) — idempotency (NTF-002). Index (`user_id`, `read_at`, `created_at DESC`).

#### 1.2. `notification_deliveries`

| Field | Type | Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| id | UUID | PK |
| notification_id | UUID | FK |
| channel | VARCHAR(8) | CHECK IN (`TELEGRAM`,`SMS`) |
| status | VARCHAR(12) | CHECK IN (`PENDING`,`SENT`,`FAILED`,`SKIPPED`) |
| attempts | SMALLINT | DEFAULT 0 |
| provider_message_id | VARCHAR(128) | NULL |
| last_error | TEXT | NULL |
| created_at / sent_at | timestamptz | |

UNIQUE (`notification_id`, `channel`).

#### 1.3. `notification_preferences`

`user_id, event_group, channel, enabled` — PK (`user_id`, `event_group`, `channel`). Сатр нест → default аз §3.

#### 1.4. `telegram_accounts`

| Field | Type | Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| user_id | UUID | PK, FK users |
| telegram_user_id | BIGINT | UNIQUE |
| chat_id | BIGINT | NOT NULL |
| username | VARCHAR(64) | NULL |
| language | VARCHAR(2) | NULL |
| linked_at | timestamptz | |
| is_blocked_by_user | BOOLEAN | DEFAULT false (403 аз Telegram) |

#### 1.5. `telegram_link_tokens`

`token_hash CHAR(64) PK, user_id, expires_at (10 дақ), used_at NULL, created_at`.

#### 1.6. `telegram_updates`

`update_id BIGINT PK, received_at` — idempotency-и webhook (TG-003); cleanup баъд аз 7 рӯз.

---

### 2. Pipeline

```text
outbox_events (PENDING)
   → dispatch_outbox (P00) → consumer "notifications"
      → RecipientResolver(event) → [user_id…]
      → барои ҳар user: INSERT notifications ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING
      → каналҳо аз preferences → notification_deliveries (PENDING)
      → Celery queue "notifications": send_telegram / send_sms (retry: 3, backoff 30s/2m/10m)
```

| ID | Қоида |
|---|---|
| NTF-001 | Notification ҳеҷ гоҳ дар transaction-и business фиристода намешавад — танҳо аз outbox (DEC-22). |
| NTF-002 | Коркарди такрории ҳамон event notification-и дуюм намесозад (UNIQUE). |
| NTF-003 | IN_APP ҳамеша сохта мешавад (хомӯш намешавад). |
| NTF-004 | Ноком шудани TELEGRAM/SMS business-ро вайрон намекунад; `FAILED` пас аз 3 кӯшиш; дар admin намоён (P12). |
| NTF-005 | Recipient-ҳо танҳо user-ҳои дорои membership-и ACTIVE ва permission-и мувофиқ дар лаҳзаи коркард. |
| NTF-006 | Матн дар забони user (`users.language`); параметрҳо (рақами order, маблағ) формат мешаванд (GLOBAL §12). |
| NTF-007 | Маълумоти махфӣ дар `params` нест. Истисно: delivery code — consumer онро дар лаҳзаи фиристодан аз `code_encrypted` мегирад ва **танҳо** ба payload-и Telegram/SMS мегузорад, на ба `notifications.params` (DEL-012). |
| NTF-008 | Quiet hours нест дар MVP; вале SMS танҳо барои event-ҳои `critical` (§3). |

---

### 3. Event → Recipients → Channels

`event_group` барои preferences. Default: ✓ = фаъол, ○ = хомӯш, ✗ = дастнорас.

| Event | Recipients | Group | Telegram | SMS |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `INVITATION_CREATED` | phone-и даъватшуда (SMS мустақим, бе user) | — | ✗ | ✓ critical |
| `VERIFICATION_SUBMITTED` | SUPERADMIN-ҳо | admin | ✓ | ✗ |
| `VERIFICATION_APPROVED/REJECTED` | Org OWNER | account | ✓ | ✓ critical |
| `SUBSCRIPTION_EXPIRING`, `SUBSCRIPTION_STATUS_CHANGED` | C.OWNER (+MANAGER барои SOFT/FULL_BLOCK) | billing | ✓ | ✓ critical (GRACE, FULL_BLOCK) |
| `PLAN_CHANGE_REQUESTED` | SUPERADMIN | admin | ✓ | ✗ |
| `IMPORT_COMPLETED/FAILED` | Сохтори import | catalog | ✓ | ✗ |
| `LOW_STOCK` | C.OWNER, MANAGER, WAREHOUSE | stock | ○ | ✗ |
| `STOCK/FINANCE_RECONCILIATION_MISMATCH` | SUPERADMIN | admin | ✓ | ✗ |
| `PARTNERSHIP_INVITED/REQUESTED` | Тарафи гиранда (OWNER, MANAGER) | partners | ✓ | ○ |
| `PARTNERSHIP_ACTIVATED/SUSPENDED/TERMINATED`, `TERMS_CHANGED` | Ҳарду OWNER (+MANAGER-и company) | partners | ✓ | ○ |
| `ORDER_CREATED` | C.OWNER, MANAGER, OPERATOR; (on-behalf: S.OWNER) | orders | ✓ | ○ |
| `ORDER_CONFIRMED/REJECTED/CANCELLED` | Store: OWNER + сохтори order; Company (агар store бекор кард): OPERATOR+ | orders | ✓ | ○ |
| `ORDER_READY` | C.OWNER, MANAGER (барои планкунии delivery) | delivery | ○ | ✗ |
| `DELIVERY_ASSIGNED` | Courier | delivery | ✓ | ✗ |
| `DELIVERY_DISPATCHED` (бо код) | S.OWNER, SELLER | delivery | ✓ | ✓ critical |
| `DELIVERY_COMPLETED/FAILED` | Store OWNER; Company MANAGER | delivery | ✓ | ○ |
| `DELIVERY_CODE_LOCKED`, `DELIVERY_MANUAL_CONFIRMED` | C.OWNER, MANAGER; (manual → S.OWNER) | delivery | ✓ | ✗ |
| `PAYMENT_RECORDED` | C.OWNER, MANAGER | finance | ✓ | ○ |
| `PAYMENT_CONFIRMED/REJECTED` | S.OWNER | finance | ✓ | ○ |
| `ADJUSTMENT_PENDING_APPROVAL` | C.OWNER | finance | ✓ | ✗ |
| `ADJUSTMENT_APPROVED` | S.OWNER | finance | ✓ | ○ |
| `DEBT_DUE_SOON`, `DEBT_OVERDUE` | S.OWNER; (overdue → C.MANAGER ҳам) | finance | ✓ | ○ |
| `RETURN_*` | Тарафи дигар (OWNER, MANAGER; RECEIVED → WAREHOUSE) | returns | ✓ | ✗ |
| `DISPUTE_*`, `DISPUTE_SLA_WARNING` | Тарафи дигар (OWNER, MANAGER) | disputes | ✓ | ✗ |

`critical` — SMS default фаъол ва user онро хомӯш карда наметавонад. Агар Telegram пайваст нест, Telegram `SKIPPED`.

---

### 4. SMS

| ID | Қоида |
|---|---|
| NTF-020 | `SmsPort` (P01) — implementation-и воқеӣ: адаптери provider-и SMS-и маҳаллӣ (HTTP API), тавассути `SMS_PROVIDER` интихоб мешавад; `ConsoleSmsProvider` дар dev/test. |
| NTF-021 | Timeout 5 с; retry танҳо барои хатоҳои network/5xx; матн ≤ 160 аломат (лотинӣ) ё 70 (кириллӣ) — шаблонҳои SMS кӯтоҳ ва алоҳида (`sms.<event>`). |
| NTF-022 | Лимит: ≤ 20 SMS/рӯз барои як phone (ба ҷуз OTP), зидди хароҷоти ногаҳонӣ. |

---

### 5. Telegram bot

#### 5.1. Webhook

| ID | Қоида |
|---|---|
| TG-001 | Endpoint: `POST /api/v1/telegram/webhook/{TELEGRAM_WEBHOOK_PATH_TOKEN}`; header `X-Telegram-Bot-Api-Secret-Token` = `TELEGRAM_WEBHOOK_SECRET` (compare_digest) → вагарна `401 webhook_unauthorized`. |
| TG-002 | Ҳамеша зуд `200` бармегардонад; коркард дар ҳамон request (амалҳо сабук ва танҳо хондан). |
| TG-003 | Idempotency: `update_id` дар `telegram_updates` (INSERT ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING → агар мавҷуд — skip). |
| TG-004 | Rate limit `telegram_webhook`. |
| TG-005 | `setWebhook` тавассути CLI: `python -m app.cli telegram-set-webhook`. |

#### 5.2. Пайвасти ҳисоб

```text
Web: Profile → «Пайваст кардани Telegram» → POST /telegram/link-token
     → {deep_link: "https://t.me/<bot>?start=<token>", expires_at}
Bot: /start <token> → hash(token) → ёфт, мӯҳлат нагузашта, истифода нашуда
     → telegram_accounts (upsert) → used_at → «Ҳисоб пайваст шуд»
```

| ID | Қоида |
|---|---|
| TG-010 | Token: 32 байт random (base64url), дар DB танҳо SHA-256; 10 дақиқа; як истифода. |
| TG-011 | Як `telegram_user_id` — як user (`telegram_already_linked` агар ба user-и дигар пайваст бошад). |
| TG-012 | Ҷудо кардан: аз web (`DELETE /telegram/link`) ё `/unlink` дар bot. |
| TG-013 | Ҳар фармони bot user-ро аз `telegram_user_id` муайян мекунад; user BLOCKED ё пайвастнашуда → паёми «Аввал ҳисобро пайваст кунед». |

#### 5.3. Фармонҳо (танҳо хондан — DEC-23)

| Фармон | Кӣ | Натиҷа |
|---|---|---|
| `/start` | ҳама | Салом + дастур |
| `/org` | ҳама | Интихоби organization (inline keyboard) — дар state-и bot (Redis, 24 соат) |
| `/orders` | Company OPERATOR+, Store OWNER/SELLER | 5 order-и охирин: рақам, ҳолат, ҷамъ + тугмаи «Кушодан дар web» |
| `/order <рақам>` | ҳамон | Тафсилот (бе нарх барои WAREHOUSE) |
| `/debt` | S.OWNER; C.OWNER/MANAGER | Store: баланс ба ҳар company; Company: ҷамъи дебиторӣ ва overdue |
| `/code` | S.OWNER/SELLER | Кодҳои delivery-ҳои IN_TRANSIT/ARRIVED |
| `/today` | Courier | Stops-и имрӯза (хондан) |
| `/lang` | ҳама | tg / ru / en |
| `/unlink` | ҳама | |

Ҳамаи маълумот тавассути service-ҳои мавҷуда бо ҳамон permission-ҳо (SEC-001), на query-и мустақим.

---

### 6. API

| Method | Path | Тавсиф |
|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/v1/notifications` | filter `unread=true`; pagination |
| GET | `/api/v1/notifications/unread-count` | |
| POST | `/api/v1/notifications/{id}/read` | |
| POST | `/api/v1/notifications/read-all` | |
| GET | `/api/v1/notifications/preferences` | Ҳама group × channel бо `locked` барои critical |
| PUT | `/api/v1/notifications/preferences` | `[{event_group, channel, enabled}]` |
| POST | `/api/v1/telegram/link-token` | |
| GET | `/api/v1/telegram/link` | Ҳолат |
| DELETE | `/api/v1/telegram/link` | |
| POST | `/api/v1/telegram/webhook/{path_token}` | Telegram |

Notification-ҳо user-scoped ҳастанд (на `X-Org-Id`); ҳар user танҳо notification-ҳои худро мебинад.

## [P12_reports_exports_admin] P12 — REPORTS, EXPORTS & ADMIN

**Вобастагӣ:** P00–P11
**Мақсад:** ҳисоботҳо барои company ва store, export-и асинхронӣ (CSV/XLSX), панели пурраи SUPERADMIN.

---

### 1. Reports

#### 1.1. Қоидаҳо

| ID | Қоида |
|---|---|
| RPT-001 | Ҳар ҳисобот танҳо дар scope-и org-и ҷорӣ (company ё store). |
| RPT-002 | Рақамҳои молиявӣ **танҳо** аз `FinanceService` / `ledger_entries` / `charges` (FIN-005) — ҳисоби параллелӣ аз orders манъ. |
| RPT-003 | Давра: `date_from`, `date_to` (санаи маҳаллии `Asia/Dushanbe`), ҳадди аксар 366 рӯз (`report_range_too_large`). |
| RPT-004 | Ҳисобот дар API синхронӣ, агар p95 ≤ 2 с (индексҳо ҳатмӣ); вагарна танҳо тавассути export. |
| RPT-005 | Ҳар ҳисобот дар API ва export ҳамон рақамҳоро медиҳад (як query builder). |

#### 1.2. Рӯйхати ҳисоботҳо

| Code | Кӣ | Мазмун | Манбаъ |
|---|---|---|---|
| `sales_summary` | Company OWNER/MANAGER | Фурӯш аз рӯи рӯз/ҳафта/моҳ: шумораи order-ҳо, ҷамъи `total` (DELIVERED+), discount, delivery fee | orders (delivered_at) |
| `sales_by_store` | ҳамон | Ҳар store: order-ҳо, фурӯш, return credit, фурӯши соф | orders + credit_notes |
| `sales_by_product` | ҳамон | Ҳар product: миқдор (base), фурӯш, return | order_items + return_items |
| `order_funnel` | Company OWNER/MANAGER/OPERATOR | Шумора аз рӯи status; % partial/rejected/cancelled; вақти миёнаи NEW→CONFIRMED | orders + history |
| `receivables_aging` | Company OWNER/MANAGER | Ҳар store: current, 1–30, 31–60, 61–90, 90+ | FinanceService |
| `payments` | Company OWNER/MANAGER | Пардохтҳо аз рӯи усул, ҳолат, рӯз | payments |
| `returns` | Company OWNER/MANAGER | Аз рӯи сабаб, product, store | returns |
| `delivery_performance` | Company OWNER/MANAGER | Аз рӯи courier: delivered, failed (+сабаб), manual override %, вақти миёнаи dispatch→delivered | deliveries |
| `inventory_snapshot` | Company OWNER/MANAGER/WAREHOUSE | Stock ҷорӣ, low stock | stocks |
| `inventory_movements` | ҳамон | Ҷамъи movements аз рӯи навъ дар давра | stock_movements |
| `purchases` | Store OWNER | Харид аз рӯи company ва моҳ | orders |
| `store_debt` | Store OWNER | Баланс ва overdue ба ҳар company | FinanceService |

#### 1.3. Dashboard-ҳо

| Dashboard | Мазмун |
|---|---|
| Company (`/company`) | Order-ҳои нав (шумора), имрӯз барои delivery, фурӯши ин моҳ, дебиторӣ ва overdue, low stock, пардохтҳо барои тасдиқ, dispute-ҳои кушода |
| Store (`/store`) | Order-ҳои фаъол, delivery-ҳои имрӯза (бо код), қарзи умумӣ, overdue |

---

### 2. Exports

#### 2.1. `exports`

| Field | Type | Constraints |
|---|---|---|
| id | UUID | PK |
| organization_id | UUID | NULL (admin export) |
| requested_by | UUID | FK |
| kind | VARCHAR(32) | report code ё `orders`, `products`, `prices`, `stock`, `statement`, `audit_logs` (admin) |
| format | VARCHAR(4) | `CSV`, `XLSX` |
| params | JSONB | filter-ҳо |
| status | VARCHAR(12) | CHECK IN (`PENDING`,`RUNNING`,`READY`,`FAILED`,`EXPIRED`) |
| file_id | UUID | NULL FK stored_files |
| row_count | INTEGER | NULL |
| error | TEXT | NULL |
| expires_at | timestamptz | `ready_at + 7 рӯз` |
| created_at / started_at / ready_at | timestamptz | |

#### 2.2. Қоидаҳо

| ID | Қоида |
|---|---|
| EXP-001 | `POST /exports` → 202; task дар queue `heavy`; permission ҳамон permission-и маълумот (масалан `statement` → `finance.view`). |
| EXP-002 | Permission ҳангоми **сохтан** ва ҳангоми **гирифтани URL** санҷида мешавад (агар membership revoke шуда бошад → 404). |
| EXP-003 | Файл дар private bucket; `GET /exports/{id}/download` → signed URL 15 дақиқа; audit `export.downloaded`. |
| EXP-004 | Файл пас аз 7 рӯз нест карда мешавад (Celery ҳаррӯза), status → `EXPIRED` (`410 export_expired`). |
| EXP-005 | Ҳадди аксар 200 000 сатр; streaming ба файл (openpyxl write-only); вақт ≤ 10 дақ, вагарна FAILED. |
| EXP-006 | CSV: UTF-8 бо BOM, ҷудокунанда `;`; пул бо нуқта (`1250.00`); XLSX: пул ҳамчун рақам бо формати `#,##0.00`. |
| EXP-007 | Сарлавҳаҳо дар забони user. |
| EXP-008 | Rate limit `export_create`. Event `EXPORT_READY` → notification. |

---

### 3. Admin (SUPERADMIN)

Ҳамаи endpoint-ҳо `/api/v1/admin/*`, dependency `require_superadmin`; ҳар амали тағйирдиҳанда `reason` талаб мекунад (`override_reason_required`) ва audit бо `actor_type = SUPERADMIN`.

| ID | Функсия | Тавсиф |
|---|---|---|
| ADM-001 | Dashboard | Шумораи company/store (аз рӯи status), subscriptions аз рӯи status, MRR (ҷамъи `price_monthly` барои ACTIVE), verification-ҳои навбат, outbox FAILED, reconciliation issues, SMS/Telegram failed (24 соат) |
| ADM-002 | Users | Ҷустуҷӯ (phone, ном), detail (memberships), block/unblock (IAM-009), logout-all |
| ADM-003 | Organizations | Ҷустуҷӯ, detail (профил, members, subscription, partnerships шумора), suspend/block/activate (P02 §2.2), иваз кардани OWNER (`transfer-ownership`: OWNER-и кӯҳна → MANAGER ё REVOKED), таҳрири legal field-ҳо (ORG-005) |
| ADM-004 | Verifications | (P02) |
| ADM-005 | Subscriptions, plans, plan requests | (P03) |
| ADM-006 | Audit viewer | Ҷустуҷӯ аз рӯи org, actor, action, entity, давра; export (CSV) |
| ADM-007 | Outbox | Рӯйхати FAILED бо хато; «аз нав кӯшидан» (→ PENDING, attempts 0) |
| ADM-008 | Notification failures | `notification_deliveries` FAILED |
| ADM-009 | Reconciliation issues | (P09) рӯйхат, resolve бо note |
| ADM-010 | Хондани маълумоти business | SUPERADMIN orders/ledger-и ҳар org-ро танҳо **хонда** метавонад (барои support); ҳар хондан audit `admin.viewed` (entity + reason-и кӯтоҳ дар query `?reason=`) |
| ADM-011 | Impersonation | **Нест** дар MVP (хатари security). |

| GET | `/api/v1/admin/organizations/{id}/orders`, `/admin/partnerships/{id}/statement` (read-only) |

## [P13_production_hardening] P13 — PRODUCTION HARDENING & FINAL GATE

**Вобастагӣ:** P00–P12
**Мақсад:** security testing, performance, E2E, backup/DR, CI/CD, deployment, observability, retention. Пас аз ин қисм система ба production омода аст.

---

### 1. Security tests (OPS-001)

Ҳар банд — тести автоматӣ дар `tests/security/`:

| # | Сенария | Натиҷаи интизорӣ |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | IDOR: ҳар endpoint-и detail бо ID-и org-и дигар (генератсияи автоматӣ аз OpenAPI) | 404 |
| 2 | Role escalation: ҳар endpoint × ҳар role-и бе permission | 403 |
| 3 | Self role change, grant OWNER тавассути API | 403/409 |
| 4 | Cross-org: `X-Org-Id`-и org-е, ки user member нест | 404/403 |
| 5 | Revoked/suspended membership | 403 фавран |
| 6 | Expired/invalid/tampered JWT; `alg=none`; refresh ҳамчун access | 401 |
| 7 | Refresh reuse | 401 + family revoked |
| 8 | Rate limits (ҳамаи §4.1 P00) | 429 |
| 9 | File: MIME spoof, андозаи калон, path traversal дар ном, polyglot | 422; калид аз ном вобаста нест |
| 10 | Export-и бе permission ва download пас аз revoke | 403/404 |
| 11 | SQL injection дар search/filter/ordering (fuzz) | 422 ё натиҷаи бехатар |
| 12 | Mass assignment: field-ҳои иловагӣ дар body (`status`, `company_id`, `allocated_amount`) | 422 (`extra="forbid"` дар ҳамаи request schema-ҳо) |
| 13 | Delivery code brute force | lock баъд аз 5 |
| 14 | Webhook бе secret | 401 |
| 15 | Маълумоти махфӣ дар logs (password, token, OTP, code) — санҷиш бо caplog дар тамоми suite | нест |
| 16 | CSRF дар `/auth/refresh` бе header | 403 |
| 17 | Ledger/stock/audit mutation бо SQL-и мустақим | exception (trigger) |

Иловагӣ: `pip-audit` ва `npm audit --audit-level=high` дар CI (build ноком агар high/critical); `bandit` барои backend; OWASP ZAP baseline scan бар staging (ҳар release).

---

### 2. Performance (OPS-010)

| ID | Талабот |
|---|---|
| OPS-010 | Seed-и load: 50 company, 5 000 store, 200 000 order, 1 000 000 ledger entry, 20 000 product. |
| OPS-011 | Locust сенарияҳо: browse catalog, checkout, confirm, payment confirm, list orders, statement. Targets — GLOBAL §16 дар 100 user-и ҳамзамон. |
| OPS-012 | `EXPLAIN ANALYZE` барои ҳар list endpoint дар seed-и load — бе Seq Scan дар ҷадвалҳои калон. |
| OPS-013 | N+1: тести шумориши query-ҳо (SQLAlchemy event) барои ҳар list endpoint — ≤ 5 query новобаста аз limit. |
| OPS-014 | Connection pool: 4 uvicorn worker × (10+20) ≤ `max_connections` − резерв барои Celery; PgBouncer (transaction mode) дар production. |

---

### 3. E2E (Playwright) — 6 flow-и critical (OPS-020)

1. **Onboarding:** register company → verification → admin approve → register store → verification → approve → partnership (invite + terms) → accept.
2. **First business flow (§145 v3):** product + unit + нарх + stock → store order → partial confirm → assemble → ready → run → courier код → delivered → charge → store payment → confirm → баланс дуруст дар ҳарду тараф.
3. **Credit block:** order аз лимит зиёд → блок → override бо reason → audit.
4. **Offline courier:** offline → arrive + confirm + cash → online → sync → ҳолатҳо дуруст; такрори sync → DUPLICATE.
5. **Return & dispute:** return → complete → credit note; dispute → adjustment (MANAGER) → OWNER approve → order COMPLETED.
6. **Subscription:** trial тамом (вақти сохта) → GRACE → SOFT_BLOCK: store order сохта наметавонад, пардохт мегузарад → admin пардохти subscription → ACTIVE.

Ҳар flow дар се забон як бор (tg пурра; ru/en — smoke).

---

### 4. Deployment (OPS-030)

#### 4.1. Топология (MVP)

```text
[Internet] → Nginx (TLS: Let's Encrypt, HTTP/2, security headers, gzip, static SPA)
              ├─ /api/*      → uvicorn (4 workers) × 2 container
              ├─ /metrics    → танҳо шабакаи дохилӣ
              └─ /*          → SPA (index.html fallback)
backend containers: api, celery-worker (queues default,notifications), celery-worker-heavy (heavy, concurrency 2), celery-beat (маҳз 1 instance)
PostgreSQL 16 (managed ё VM-и алоҳида) + PgBouncer
Redis 7 (persistence AOF — барои broker)
S3-compatible storage (MinIO кластер ё managed)
```

#### 4.2. Муҳитҳо

`development` (docker compose), `staging` (нусхаи production бо маълумоти сохта), `production`. Маълумоти воқеӣ ба staging/dev **намеравад**.

#### 4.3. Migrations (OPS-031)

- Expand/contract: тағйири шикананда дар ду release (1: илова + навиштан дар ҳарду; 2: хориҷ).
- `alembic upgrade head` ҳамчун job-и алоҳида **пеш аз** навсозии api; ноком → deploy қатъ.
- Ҳар migration `downgrade` дорад; дар CI `upgrade → downgrade → upgrade` санҷида мешавад.
- Migration-ҳои дароз (index) — `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY` дар migration-и алоҳида бо `autocommit_block`.

#### 4.4. CI/CD (OPS-032)

```text
PR:     lint → typecheck → unit/integration/API tests → security tests → frontend tests → build → traceability
main:   ҳамаи боло → docker images (tag = git sha) → deploy staging → migrations → smoke → E2E (Playwright)
release (tag vX.Y.Z): тасдиқи дастӣ → backup-и pre-deploy → migrations → rolling deploy api → workers → smoke → мониторинг 30 дақ
rollback: image-и қаблӣ (migration-ҳо backward-compatible аз рӯи expand/contract)
```

---

### 5. Backup ва DR (OPS-040)

| ID | Талабот |
|---|---|
| OPS-040 | PostgreSQL: WAL archiving пайваста (pgBackRest ё wal-g) ба S3-и **дигар** (региони/провайдери дигар) + full backup ҳаррӯза 02:00 + differential. |
| OPS-041 | **RPO = 15 дақиқа**, **RTO = 4 соат**. |
| OPS-042 | Backup-ҳо рамзгузорӣ (AES-256, калид дар secret manager, на дар ҳамон сервер). |
| OPS-043 | Нигоҳдорӣ: daily 30 рӯз, monthly 12 моҳ. |
| OPS-044 | Файлҳо (S3): versioning + replication ба bucket-и дигар. |
| OPS-045 | Redis аз backup барқарор карда намешавад (outbox дар PostgreSQL аст — ҳеҷ чиз гум намешавад). |
| OPS-046 | Restore test **ҳар моҳ**: барқарорӣ ба муҳити алоҳида → `alembic current` → reconciliation-и молиявӣ (FIN-060) ва stock (INV-009) бояд тоза бошад → протокол дар `docs/restore-tests/YYYY-MM.md`. |
| OPS-047 | Runbook-и DR (`docs/runbooks/dr.md`): қадамҳо, масъулон, тамос, санҷиши баъдӣ. |
| OPS-048 | Пеш аз ҳар release — backup-и дастӣ. |

---

### 6. Observability (OPS-050)

| ID | Талабот |
|---|---|
| OPS-050 | Logs JSON (structlog) → марказонидашуда (Loki ё монанд); нигоҳдорӣ 30 рӯз. |
| OPS-051 | Metrics (Prometheus): RPS, latency p50/p95/p99 аз рӯи route, 5xx, DB pool, Celery queue length ва task failures, outbox PENDING/FAILED, SMS/Telegram failures. |
| OPS-052 | Alerts: 5xx > 1% дар 5 дақ; p95 > 2× target дар 10 дақ; outbox PENDING синну сол > 5 дақ; outbox FAILED > 0; reconciliation mismatch; backup ноком; диск > 80%; сертификат < 14 рӯз. |
| OPS-053 | Sentry: backend + frontend, бо `release` ва `environment`; PII scrubbing. |
| OPS-054 | Business metrics дар admin dashboard (ADM-001). |

---

### 7. Retention (OPS-060)

| Маълумот | Мӯҳлат |
|---|---|
| Ledger, charges, credits, allocations, payments, orders, returns, disputes | Ҳеҷ гоҳ нест намешавад (ҳадди ақал 5 сол — талаботи ҳуҷҷатгузории молиявӣ) |
| audit_logs | 5 сол; баъд аз 1 сол ба partition-и архивӣ (partitioning аз рӯи моҳ) |
| Verification documents | То мавҷудияти org + 5 сол |
| outbox_events PROCESSED | 30 рӯз (архив, на delete: ҷадвали `outbox_events_archive`) |
| idempotency_records | 24 соат / 7 рӯз (sync) |
| notifications | 180 рӯз |
| courier_sync_operations | 30 рӯз |
| exports файлҳо | 7 рӯз |
| logs | 30 рӯз |

Ҷадвалҳои калон (`audit_logs`, `ledger_entries`, `stock_movements`) partitioning аз рӯи моҳ (range дар `created_at`) — дар ин қисм ҳамчун migration илова мешавад, агар маълумот > 10M сатр пешбинӣ шавад; вагарна фақат index-ҳо.

### 8. Documentation (OPS-070)

`docs/`: `architecture.md` (диаграммаи C4 L1–L2), `erd.md` (Mermaid ERD аз models генератсия — `scripts/gen_erd.py`), `runbooks/` (deploy, rollback, DR, outbox stuck, reconciliation mismatch, SMS provider down), `api.md` (линк ба OpenAPI), `onboarding.md` барои developer.

### 9. FINAL ACCEPTANCE GATE (OPS-099)

Production танҳо вақте, ки **ҳама** ✓:

```text
[ ] Ҳамаи PART_REPORT.md (P00–P12) бе банди боқимонда
[ ] Traceability: 100% requirement ID-ҳо test доранд
[ ] Coverage: service ≥ 90%, умумӣ ≥ 80%
[ ] Security suite (§1) сабз; pip-audit/npm audit бе high/critical; ZAP бе high
[ ] Performance targets (GLOBAL §16) дар seed-и load
[ ] 6 E2E flow сабз дар staging
[ ] Restore test иҷро шуд ва reconciliation тоза
[ ] Alerts санҷида шуданд (trigger-и сунъӣ)
[ ] Runbook-ҳо навишта шуданд
[ ] Тарҷумаҳо: tg 100%, ru 100%, en 100% (скрипти санҷиши калидҳо)
[ ] Superadmin сохта шуд; секретҳои production аз secret manager
[ ] Сертификат TLS, security headers (securityheaders.com ≥ A)
```
